# Plan: Add Cursor Agent Support on New Agent Interface

## Context

The `soph/agent-refactor` branch refactored the agent system from a callback-driven model (agents call framework functions) to a dispatcher-driven model (agents are passive data providers, framework calls agent methods). PR #260 attempted to add Cursor support on the *old* interface. We need to implement Cursor support on the *new* interface.

The new architecture has a single `DispatchLifecycleEvent` dispatcher that routes normalized `Event` objects from any agent. Adding a new agent means:
1. Implement the `Agent` interface (identity + `ParseHookEvent` + transcript methods)
2. Implement `HookSupport` for hook installation
3. Implement `HookHandler` (legacy, for backward compat)
4. Register in `init()` — the framework auto-discovers agents

## Branch

Create `robin/cursor-agent` from `soph/agent-refactor`.

## Files to Create

### 1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/cursor.go` — Main agent implementation

Implements the core `Agent` interface:
- **Identity:** `Name()="cursor"`, `Type()="Cursor"`, `Description()="Cursor - AI-powered code editor"`, `DetectPresence()` checks `.cursor/` dir, `ProtectedDirs()=[".cursor"]`
- **Event Mapping:**
  - `HookNames()` → `["session-start", "session-end", "before-submit-prompt", "stop", "pre-task", "post-task", "post-todo"]`
  - `ParseHookEvent()` maps hooks to lifecycle events (see mapping table below)
- **Transcript Storage:** `ReadTranscript`, `ChunkTranscript`, `ReassembleTranscript` — JSONL format (same as Claude Code, delegate to `agent.ChunkJSONL`/`agent.ReassembleJSONL`)
- **Legacy methods:** `ParseHookInput`, `GetSessionID`, `GetSessionDir`, `ResolveSessionFile`, `ReadSession`, `WriteSession`, `FormatResumeCommand`, `GetHookConfigPath`, `SupportsHooks`

Pattern to follow: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go`

### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/lifecycle.go` — Event parsing

Core `ParseHookEvent` implementation with private parse methods:
- `parseSessionStart(stdin)` → `Event{Type: SessionStart, SessionID, SessionRef}`
- `parseTurnStart(stdin)` → `Event{Type: TurnStart, SessionID, SessionRef, Prompt}`
- `parseTurnEnd(stdin)` → `Event{Type: TurnEnd, SessionID, SessionRef}`
- `parseSessionEnd(stdin)` → `Event{Type: SessionEnd, SessionID, SessionRef}`
- `parseSubagentStart(stdin)` → `Event{Type: SubagentStart, SessionID, SessionRef, ToolUseID, ToolInput}`
- `parseSubagentEnd(stdin)` → `Event{Type: SubagentEnd, SessionID, SessionRef, ToolUseID, SubagentID, ToolInput}`
- `post-todo` → `nil, nil` (no lifecycle action, handled specially like Claude)

Also implement `TranscriptAnalyzer` interface:
- `GetTranscriptPosition(path)` — line count (JSONL)
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path, startOffset)` — parse JSONL lines, extract Write/Edit tools
- `ExtractPrompts(sessionRef, fromOffset)` — extract user messages
- `ExtractSummary(sessionRef)` — last assistant text

**Key insight:** Cursor uses the same JSONL transcript format as Claude Code (confirmed by PR #260 and Cursor docs). We can reuse Claude's transcript parsing utilities from the `transcript` package.

Pattern to follow: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle.go`

### 3. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/hooks.go` — Hook installation

Implements `HookSupport` and `HookHandler` interfaces:
- `InstallHooks(localDev, force)` — writes `.cursor/hooks.json`
- `UninstallHooks()` — removes Entire entries from `.cursor/hooks.json`
- `AreHooksInstalled()` — checks for Entire hooks
- `GetSupportedHooks()` — returns supported hook types
- `GetHookNames()` — returns hook verb list (delegates to `HookNames()`)

**Cursor hooks.json format:**
```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "hooks": {
    "sessionStart": [{"command": "entire hooks cursor session-start"}],
    "sessionEnd": [{"command": "entire hooks cursor session-end"}],
    "beforeSubmitPrompt": [{"command": "entire hooks cursor before-submit-prompt"}],
    "stop": [{"command": "entire hooks cursor stop"}],
    "preToolUse": [{"command": "entire hooks cursor pre-task", "matcher": "Task"}],
    "postToolUse": [
      {"command": "entire hooks cursor post-task", "matcher": "Task"},
      {"command": "entire hooks cursor post-todo", "matcher": "TodoWrite"}
    ]
  }
}
```

Note: Cursor's preToolUse/postToolUse support `matcher` field for filtering by tool name (similar to Claude's structure). The `matcher` field is optional.

Pattern to follow: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go`

### 4. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/types.go` — Type definitions

Cursor-specific types:
- `CursorHooksFile` — root `.cursor/hooks.json` structure
- `CursorHooks` — hook arrays (camelCase keys: `sessionStart`, `sessionEnd`, `beforeSubmitPrompt`, `stop`, `preToolUse`, `postToolUse`)
- `CursorHookEntry` — `{command, matcher?}`
- stdin payload structs: `sessionInfoRaw`, `userPromptSubmitRaw`, `taskHookInputRaw`, `postToolHookInputRaw`

Cursor payloads include both `session_id` and `conversation_id` (fallback). Use `session_id` if present, fall back to `conversation_id`.

Pattern to follow: `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/types.go`

## Hook Name → Event Mapping

| Cursor Hook Name | Event Type | Notes |
|------------------|------------|-------|
| `session-start` | `SessionStart` | Same as Claude |
| `before-submit-prompt` | `TurnStart` | Cursor's name for user-prompt-submit |
| `stop` | `TurnEnd` | Same as Claude |
| `session-end` | `SessionEnd` | Same as Claude |
| `pre-task` | `SubagentStart` | Maps from preToolUse[Task] |
| `post-task` | `SubagentEnd` | Maps from postToolUse[Task] |
| `post-todo` | `nil` | Incremental checkpoint, handled directly |

## Key Design Decisions

1. **JSONL transcript format**: Cursor uses the same JSONL format as Claude Code. Reuse the `transcript` package utilities for parsing.

2. **No TranscriptPreparer**: Cursor doesn't need the sentinel-polling that Claude Code uses. The transcript is synchronously written before hooks fire.

3. **conversation_id fallback**: Cursor payloads may send `conversation_id` instead of `session_id`. Parse both, prefer `session_id`.

4. **Matcher support in hooks.json**: Cursor's hooks.json supports an optional `matcher` field on hook entries (similar to Claude's per-tool filtering). Use this for pre-task (Task matcher) and post-todo (TodoWrite matcher).

5. **No settings merge**: Unlike PR #260's `.cursor/entire.json` approach, we use `.entire/settings.json` as the single source of truth. Cursor doesn't need separate settings.

## Reusable Code

- `agent.ChunkJSONL()` / `agent.ReassembleJSONL()` from `cmd/entire/cli/agent/chunking.go`
- `transcript.ParseFromBytes()`, `transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine()` from `cmd/entire/cli/transcript/`
- `transcript.ExtractUserContent()` for prompt extraction
- `claudecode.ExtractModifiedFiles()` logic pattern (but Cursor may have different tool names)
- Generic `readAndParse[T]()` pattern from Claude/Gemini lifecycle.go

## Verification

1. `mise run fmt && mise run lint` — ensure code passes formatting and linting
2. `mise run test` — unit tests pass
3. `mise run test:integration` — integration tests pass
4. `mise run test:ci` — full CI suite
5. Manual verification:
   - `go build ./cmd/entire/` compiles
   - `go run ./cmd/entire/ enable --agent cursor` in a test repo creates `.cursor/hooks.json`
   - `go run ./cmd/entire/ disable` removes hooks from `.cursor/hooks.json`
   - Hook commands exist: `go run ./cmd/entire/ hooks cursor --help` shows all hook verbs

## Complete Agent System Architecture

### 1. Agent Interface and Core Types (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/`)

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go` -- Core Agent Interface

The `Agent` interface has **18 methods** organized into four groups:

**Identity (5 methods):**
- `Name() AgentName` -- registry key (e.g., `"claude-code"`, `"gemini"`)
- `Type() AgentType` -- display name stored in metadata (e.g., `"Claude Code"`, `"Gemini CLI"`)
- `Description() string` -- human-readable description for UI
- `DetectPresence() (bool, error)` -- checks if agent is configured in the repo (looks for `.claude/` or `.gemini/` dir)
- `ProtectedDirs() []string` -- directories never modified during rewind (e.g., `[".claude"]`)

**Event Mapping (2 methods):**
- `HookNames() []string` -- hook verb subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`
- `ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)` -- translates native hook into normalized lifecycle Event. Returns `nil` for hooks with no lifecycle significance.

**Transcript Storage (3 methods):**
- `ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)` -- reads raw transcript bytes
- `ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)` -- format-aware chunking (JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays)
- `ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)` -- reverses chunking

**Legacy (8 methods) -- will move to optional interfaces later:**
- `GetHookConfigPath() string`
- `SupportsHooks() bool`
- `ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)`
- `GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string`
- `GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)`
- `ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string`
- `ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)`
- `WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error`
- `FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string`

**Optional interfaces** (implement as needed):
- `HookSupport` -- `InstallHooks`, `UninstallHooks`, `AreHooksInstalled`, `GetSupportedHooks`
- `HookHandler` -- `GetHookNames()` (backward compat, delegates from `HookNames()`)
- `FileWatcher` -- for agents without hooks (file-based detection)
- `TranscriptAnalyzer` -- `GetTranscriptPosition`, `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset`, `ExtractPrompts`, `ExtractSummary`
- `TranscriptPreparer` -- `PrepareTranscript` (e.g., Claude waits for async flush)
- `TokenCalculator` -- `CalculateTokenUsage`
- `SubagentAwareExtractor` -- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles`, `CalculateTotalTokenUsage` (for agents with subagents)

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/types.go` -- HookType, HookInput, TokenUsage

Key types:
- `HookType` string constants: `HookSessionStart`, `HookSessionEnd`, `HookUserPromptSubmit`, `HookStop`, `HookPreToolUse`, `HookPostToolUse`
- `HookInput` struct with normalized fields: `HookType`, `SessionID`, `SessionRef`, `Timestamp`, `UserPrompt`, `ToolName`, `ToolUseID`, `ToolInput`, `ToolResponse`, `RawData`
- `TokenUsage` struct: `InputTokens`, `CacheCreationTokens`, `CacheReadTokens`, `OutputTokens`, `APICallCount`, `SubagentTokens`
- `SessionChange` struct for `FileWatcher`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/event.go` -- Lifecycle Events

`EventType` constants:
- `SessionStart`, `TurnStart`, `TurnEnd`, `Compaction`, `SessionEnd`, `SubagentStart`, `SubagentEnd`

`Event` struct fields: `Type`, `SessionID`, `PreviousSessionID`, `SessionRef`, `Prompt`, `Timestamp`, `ToolUseID`, `SubagentID`, `ToolInput`, `ResponseMessage`, `Metadata`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/registry.go` -- Agent Registration

Key pattern: **Factory-based self-registration via `init()`**

```go
var registry = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
type Factory func() Agent

func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory)
func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error)
func List() []AgentName  // sorted
func Detect() (Agent, error)  // checks DetectPresence() on each
func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error)
func AllProtectedDirs() []string  // union from all agents
func Default() Agent  // returns DefaultAgentName (claude-code)
```

Agent name/type constants:
```go
AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"

AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent"

DefaultAgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
```

For a Cursor agent, you would add:
```go
AgentNameCursor AgentName = "cursor"
AgentTypeCursor AgentType = "Cursor"
```

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/chunking.go` -- Transcript Chunking

- `MaxChunkSize = 50MB` (GitHub blob limit safety margin)
- `ChunkTranscript(content, agentType)` -- dispatches to agent-specific chunking
- `ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)` -- dispatches to agent-specific reassembly
- `ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)` -- splits JSONL at newline boundaries (default)
- `ReassembleJSONL(chunks)` -- concatenates with newlines
- `ChunkFileName(baseName, index)` / `ParseChunkIndex(filename, baseName)` / `SortChunkFiles` -- chunk file naming
- `DetectAgentTypeFromContent(content)` -- checks if content is Gemini JSON format

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/session.go` -- AgentSession

`AgentSession` struct: `SessionID`, `AgentName`, `RepoPath`, `SessionRef`, `StartTime`, `NativeData` (raw bytes in agent's native format), `ModifiedFiles`, `NewFiles`, `DeletedFiles`, `Entries`

Sessions are **not interoperable** between agents. Each agent reads/writes its own format.

### 2. Claude Code Agent (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/`)

This is the reference implementation. Files:

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/claude.go` -- Main Implementation

- Self-registers in `init()`: `agent.Register(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode, NewClaudeCodeAgent)`
- Struct: `ClaudeCodeAgent struct{}` (empty, stateless)
- `DetectPresence()` checks for `.claude/` directory at repo root
- `GetSessionDir()` returns `~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-path>/`
- `ResolveSessionFile()` returns `<dir>/<id>.jsonl`
- Transcript format: **JSONL** (one JSON object per line)
- `GetTranscriptPosition()` counts lines (lightweight, no JSON parse)
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` parses JSONL from offset, extracts files from Write/Edit tools
- `ChunkTranscript()` / `ReassembleTranscript()` delegate to `agent.ChunkJSONL` / `agent.ReassembleJSONL`
- `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` and `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` include subagent transcripts
- `FormatResumeCommand()` returns `"claude -r " + sessionID`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/types.go` -- Claude-Specific Types

- `ClaudeSettings`, `ClaudeHooks`, `ClaudeHookMatcher`, `ClaudeHookEntry` -- settings.json structure
- `sessionInfoRaw`, `userPromptSubmitRaw`, `taskHookInputRaw`, `postToolHookInputRaw` -- raw JSON from hooks
- File modification tool names: `Write`, `Edit`, `NotebookEdit`, `mcp__acp__Write`, `mcp__acp__Edit`
- `messageUsage` / `messageWithUsage` -- for token calculation

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go` -- Hook Installation

- Implements `HookSupport` and `HookHandler` interfaces
- `GetHookNames()` returns 7 hooks: `session-start`, `session-end`, `stop`, `user-prompt-submit`, `pre-task`, `post-task`, `post-todo`
- `InstallHooks()` reads/modifies `.claude/settings.json` preserving unknown fields
- Also adds `permissions.deny` rule to block Claude from reading metadata
- Commands use form: `"entire hooks claude-code <verb>"` (or `"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/..."` for localDev)
- `entireHookPrefixes` used to identify Entire hooks
- `UninstallHooks()` removes only Entire hooks, preserving user hooks
- `AreHooksInstalled()` checks for known command patterns

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` -- Transcript Parsing

- `TranscriptLine` is an alias for `transcript.Line`
- `ExtractModifiedFiles()` -- scans assistant messages for tool_use blocks with file modification tools
- `ExtractLastUserPrompt()` -- handles both string and array content formats
- `TruncateAtUUID()` -- returns lines up to UUID
- `FindCheckpointUUID()` -- finds tool_result for a given tool_use_id
- `CalculateTokenUsage()` -- deduplicates by message.id (handles streaming duplicates)
- `ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs()` -- finds "agentId: <id>" in Task tool results
- `CalculateTotalTokenUsage()` / `ExtractAllModifiedFiles()` -- include subagent data

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/lifecycle.go` -- Lifecycle Event Parsing

- Compile-time assertions: implements `TranscriptAnalyzer`, `TranscriptPreparer`, `TokenCalculator`, `SubagentAwareExtractor`
- `HookNames()` delegates to `GetHookNames()`
- `ParseHookEvent()` -- the key switch statement mapping hook names to event types:
  - `session-start` -> `SessionStart`
  - `user-prompt-submit` -> `TurnStart`
  - `stop` -> `TurnEnd`
  - `session-end` -> `SessionEnd`
  - `pre-task` -> `SubagentStart`
  - `post-task` -> `SubagentEnd`
  - `post-todo` -> `nil` (handled separately)
- `readAndParse[T]()` -- generic helper to read stdin and unmarshal JSON
- `PrepareTranscript()` -- waits for Claude's async transcript flush (polls for sentinel)
- `waitForTranscriptFlush()` -- polls transcript tail for `stopHookSentinel` within 3s
- `ExtractPrompts()`, `ExtractSummary()` -- delegate to transcript parsing

### 3. Gemini CLI Agent (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/`)

This is the second implementation, useful as a reference for a different transcript format.

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/gemini.go` -- Main Implementation

- Self-registers: `agent.Register(agent.AgentNameGemini, NewGeminiCLIAgent)`
- `DetectPresence()` checks for `.gemini/` directory
- `GetSessionDir()` returns `~/.gemini/tmp/<sanitized-path>/chats/`
- `ResolveSessionFile()` uses pattern `session-*-<first8chars>.json` (Gemini's naming convention)
- Transcript format: **JSON** with `{"messages": [...]}` structure (NOT JSONL)
- `GetTranscriptPosition()` returns message count (not line count)
- `ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset()` iterates messages from offset
- File modification tools: `write_file`, `edit_file`, `save_file`, `replace`
- `ChunkTranscript()` splits messages array preserving JSON structure
- `ReassembleTranscript()` merges message arrays from chunks
- `FormatResumeCommand()` returns `"gemini --resume " + sessionID`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/types.go` -- Gemini-Specific Types

- `GeminiSettings`, `GeminiHooksConfig`, `GeminiHooks`, `GeminiHookMatcher`, `GeminiHookEntry`
- Notable: `GeminiHookEntry` has a `Name` field (required, unlike Claude)
- Hook type names use different convention: `SessionStart`, `BeforeAgent`, `AfterAgent`, `BeforeModel`, `AfterModel`, `BeforeToolSelection`, `BeforeTool`, `AfterTool`, `PreCompress`, `Notification`
- `sessionInfoRaw` has extra fields: `Cwd`, `HookEventName`, `Source`, `Reason`
- `geminiMessageTokens` with `Input`, `Output`, `Cached`, `Thoughts`, `Tool`, `Total`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/hooks.go` -- Hook Installation

- 11 hook names (more than Claude's 7): `session-start`, `session-end`, `before-agent`, `after-agent`, `before-model`, `after-model`, `before-tool-selection`, `before-tool`, `after-tool`, `pre-compress`, `notification`
- `InstallHooks()` sets `hooksConfig.enabled = true` (Gemini requires this)
- Installs 12 hooks total (session-end installs two: exit + logout matchers)
- Uses `entireHookPrefixes` with `"entire "` and `"go run ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}/..."`
- Has idempotency check (returns 0 if same hooks already installed)

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/lifecycle.go` -- Lifecycle Event Parsing

- Implements `TranscriptAnalyzer`, `TokenCalculator`
- Does NOT implement `TranscriptPreparer` or `SubagentAwareExtractor`
- `ParseHookEvent()` mappings:
  - `session-start` -> `SessionStart`
  - `before-agent` -> `TurnStart` (Gemini's equivalent of UserPromptSubmit)
  - `after-agent` -> `TurnEnd` (Gemini's equivalent of Stop)
  - `session-end` -> `SessionEnd`
  - `pre-compress` -> `Compaction` (unique to Gemini)
  - `before-tool`, `after-tool`, `before-model`, `after-model`, `before-tool-selection`, `notification` -> `nil` (no lifecycle action)
- `readAndParse[T]()` -- same generic pattern as Claude

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli/transcript.go` -- Transcript Parsing

- `GeminiTranscript` struct with `Messages []GeminiMessage`
- `GeminiMessage` has custom `UnmarshalJSON` to handle both string and array content
- `GeminiToolCall` with `Args map[string]interface{}`
- File path extraction checks `file_path`, `path`, `filename` args
- `SliceFromMessage()` -- equivalent of `transcript.SliceFromLine` but by message index
- `CalculateTokenUsage()` / `CalculateTokenUsageFromFile()` -- processes `tokens` object per message

The "(Preview)" label for Gemini appears in `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` at line 620-629:

if agentName == agent.AgentNameGemini {
    msg += " (Preview)"
}
```

### 4. Hook System

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cmd.go` -- Hook Command Tree

- Creates `entire hooks` command (hidden, internal)
- Adds `entire hooks git` subcommand for strategy-level git hooks
- **Dynamically adds agent subcommands**: iterates `agent.List()`, casts to `HookHandler`, calls `newAgentHooksCmd()`
- Imports agents to ensure registration: `_ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"` and `_ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"`

**For a new Cursor agent, you would add:**
```go
_ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor"
```

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/hook_registry.go` -- Hook Dispatch

- `currentHookAgentName` -- tracks which agent is running the current hook
- `GetCurrentHookAgent()` -- returns agent from hook context
- `newAgentHooksCmd()` -- creates `entire hooks <agentName>` command with logging setup
- `newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging()` -- creates individual verb commands (e.g., `entire hooks claude-code stop`)
- **Critical dispatch logic:**
  1. Checks if in git repo and Entire is enabled (silent skip if not)
  2. Gets agent via `agent.Get(agentName)`
  3. Calls `ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, cmd.InOrStdin())` to get normalized Event
  4. If event is non-nil: calls `DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)` (generic dispatcher)
  5. Special case: Claude's `post-todo` is handled directly via `handleClaudeCodePostTodo()`
  6. All other nil events are no-ops

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/lifecycle.go` -- Generic Lifecycle Dispatcher

`DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)` routes events to handlers:
- `SessionStart` -> shows banner, checks concurrent sessions, fires state machine
- `TurnStart` -> captures pre-prompt state (including transcript position), ensures strategy setup, initializes session
- `TurnEnd` -> validates transcript, copies it to metadata dir, extracts prompts/summary/modified files, detects git status changes, calculates tokens, calls `strat.SaveStep()`, transitions phase
- `Compaction` -> resets transcript offset, stays in ACTIVE phase
- `SessionEnd` -> marks session as ended
- `SubagentStart` -> captures pre-task state
- `SubagentEnd` -> detects changes, saves task checkpoint via `strat.SaveTaskStep()`

---

### 5. Transcript Package (`cmd/entire/cli/transcript/`)

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript/types.go` -- Shared Types

Claude-specific JSONL types used by both the transcript package and claudecode package:
```go
type Line struct {
    Type    string          `json:"type"`    // "user" or "assistant"
    UUID    string          `json:"uuid"`
    Message json.RawMessage `json:"message"`
}
type UserMessage struct { Content interface{} }
type AssistantMessage struct { Content []ContentBlock }
type ContentBlock struct { Type, Text, Name string; Input json.RawMessage }
type ToolInput struct { FilePath, NotebookPath, Description, Command, Pattern, Skill, URL, Prompt string }
```

Constants: `TypeUser = "user"`, `TypeAssistant = "assistant"`, `ContentTypeText = "text"`, `ContentTypeToolUse = "tool_use"`

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript/parse.go` -- Parsing Logic

- `ParseFromBytes(content)` -- parses JSONL byte slice into `[]Line`
- `ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)` -- reads file starting from line offset, returns `([]Line, totalLines, error)`
- `SliceFromLine(content, startLine)` -- returns byte content starting from line offset
- `ExtractUserContent(message)` -- extracts text from user messages (handles string and array formats), strips IDE context tags

---

### 6. Setup Flow (`cmd/entire/cli/setup.go`)

#### `/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/setup.go` -- Enable/Disable Commands

**`entire enable` command** supports:
- `--agent <name>` -- non-interactive mode for specific agent
- `--strategy <name>` -- manual-commit or auto-commit
- `--local-dev` -- use `go run` instead of binary
- `--force` / `-f` -- force reinstall hooks
- `--skip-push-sessions` -- disable session log pushing
- `--telemetry` -- enable/disable analytics

**Enable flow:**
1. Check git repository
2. Detect/select agent
3. Install agent hooks (`ag.(HookSupport).InstallHooks()`)
4. Create `.entire/` directory with `.gitignore`
5. Save settings (strategy, enabled flag, etc.)
6. Install git hooks (`strategy.InstallGitHook()`)
7. Prompt telemetry consent (interactive mode)
8. Call `strat.EnsureSetup()`

**`setupAgentHooksNonInteractive()`** is the key function for `--agent` mode:
- Casts to `HookSupport`, installs hooks
- Appends "(Preview)" suffix for Gemini agent
- Saves settings, installs git hooks, calls strategy setup

**`isFullyEnabled()`** checks: settings exist + enabled, any agent hooks installed, git hooks installed, `.entire/` dir exists.

**`entire disable --uninstall`** removes: agent hooks (all registered), git hooks, session states, `.entire/` directory, shadow branches.

---

### Key Patterns for Implementing a Cursor Agent

1. **Create package** `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/` with files following the same structure:
   - `cursor.go` -- main implementation with `init()` self-registration
   - `types.go` -- Cursor-specific types (settings structure, hook input formats, file modification tool names)
   - `hooks.go` -- `HookSupport`/`HookHandler` implementation (install/uninstall/check hooks)
   - `transcript.go` -- Cursor transcript parsing (format depends on what Cursor uses)
   - `lifecycle.go` -- `ParseHookEvent()` mapping Cursor hooks to lifecycle events, optional interface implementations

2. **Register constants** in `registry.go`:
   ```go
   AgentNameCursor AgentName = "cursor"
   AgentTypeCursor AgentType = "Cursor"
   ```

3. **Import for side-effect registration** in `hooks_cmd.go`:
   ```go
   _ "github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor"
   ```

4. **Implement the Agent interface** -- all 18 methods plus `HookSupport` and `HookHandler`

5. **Map Cursor's hook names** to lifecycle events in `ParseHookEvent()`. The minimum required events are: `SessionStart`, `TurnStart`, `TurnEnd`, `SessionEnd`

6. **Handle Cursor's transcript format** -- implement `ReadTranscript`, `ChunkTranscript`, `ReassembleTranscript` and optionally `TranscriptAnalyzer` if you want rich metadata extraction

7. **Update setup.go** -- add Cursor to `removeAgentHooks()`, `checkCursorHooksInstalled()`, and potentially add `(Preview)` label like Gemini

8. **Add compile-time interface assertions** like both existing agents do:
   ```go
   var _ agent.HookSupport = (*CursorAgent)(nil)
   ```

     1→package agent
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"fmt"
     5→	"slices"
     6→	"sync"
     7→)
     8→
     9→var (
    10→	registryMu sync.RWMutex
    11→	registry   = make(map[AgentName]Factory)
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Factory creates a new agent instance
    15→type Factory func() Agent
    16→
    17→// Register adds an agent factory to the registry.
    18→// Called from init() in each agent implementation.
    19→func Register(name AgentName, factory Factory) {
    20→	registryMu.Lock()
    21→	defer registryMu.Unlock()
    22→	registry[name] = factory
    23→}
    24→
    25→// Get retrieves an agent by name.
    26→//
    27→
    28→func Get(name AgentName) (Agent, error) {
    29→	registryMu.RLock()
    30→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    31→
    32→	factory, ok := registry[name]
    33→	if !ok {
    34→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent: %s (available: %v)", name, List())
    35→	}
    36→	return factory(), nil
    37→}
    38→
    39→// List returns all registered agent names in sorted order.
    40→func List() []AgentName {
    41→	registryMu.RLock()
    42→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    43→
    44→	names := make([]AgentName, 0, len(registry))
    45→	for name := range registry {
    46→		names = append(names, name)
    47→	}
    48→	slices.Sort(names)
    49→	return names
    50→}
    51→
    52→// Detect attempts to auto-detect which agent is being used.
    53→// Checks each registered agent's DetectPresence method.
    54→//
    55→
    56→func Detect() (Agent, error) {
    57→	registryMu.RLock()
    58→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
    59→
    60→	for _, factory := range registry {
    61→		ag := factory()
    62→		if present, err := ag.DetectPresence(); err == nil && present {
    63→			return ag, nil
    64→		}
    65→	}
    66→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("no agent detected (available: %v)", List())
    67→}
    68→
    69→// AgentName is the registry key type for agents (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini").
    70→//
    71→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentType when both are used
    72→type AgentName string
    73→
    74→// AgentType is the display name type stored in metadata/trailers (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI").
    75→//
    76→//nolint:revive // stuttering is intentional - distinguishes from AgentName when both are used
    77→type AgentType string
    78→
    79→// Agent name constants (registry keys)
    80→const (
    81→	AgentNameClaudeCode AgentName = "claude-code"
    82→	AgentNameGemini     AgentName = "gemini"
    83→)
    84→
    85→// Agent type constants (type identifiers stored in metadata/trailers)
    86→const (
    87→	AgentTypeClaudeCode AgentType = "Claude Code"
    88→	AgentTypeGemini     AgentType = "Gemini CLI"
    89→	AgentTypeUnknown    AgentType = "Agent" // Fallback for backwards compatibility
    90→)
    91→
    92→// DefaultAgentName is the registry key for the default agent.
    93→const DefaultAgentName AgentName = AgentNameClaudeCode
    94→
    95→// GetByAgentType retrieves an agent by its type identifier.
    96→//
    97→// Note: This uses a linear search that instantiates agents until a match is found.
    98→// This is acceptable because:
    99→//   - Agent count is small (~2-20 agents)
   100→//   - Agent factories are lightweight (empty struct allocation)
   101→//   - Called infrequently (commit hooks, rewind, debug commands - not hot paths)
   102→//   - Cost is ~400ns worst case vs milliseconds for I/O operations
   103→//
   104→// Only optimize if agent count exceeds 100 or profiling shows this as a bottleneck.
   105→func GetByAgentType(agentType AgentType) (Agent, error) {
   106→	registryMu.RLock()
   107→	defer registryMu.RUnlock()
   108→
   109→	for _, factory := range registry {
   110→		ag := factory()
   111→		if ag.Type() == agentType {
   112→			return ag, nil
   113→		}
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("unknown agent type: %s", agentType)
   117→}
   118→
   119→// AllProtectedDirs returns the union of ProtectedDirs from all registered agents.
   120→func AllProtectedDirs() []string {
   121→	// Copy factories under the lock, then release before calling external code.
   122→	registryMu.RLock()
   123→	factories := make([]Factory, 0, len(registry))
   124→	for _, f := range registry {
   125→		factories = append(factories, f)
   126→	}
   127→	registryMu.RUnlock()
   128→
   129→	seen := make(map[string]struct{})
   130→	var dirs []string
   131→	for _, factory := range factories {
   132→		for _, d := range factory().ProtectedDirs() {
   133→			if _, ok := seen[d]; !ok {
   134→				seen[d] = struct{}{}
   135→				dirs = append(dirs, d)
   136→			}
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→	slices.Sort(dirs)
   140→	return dirs
   141→}
   142→
   143→// Default returns the default agent.
   144→// Returns nil if the default agent is not registered.
   145→//
   146→//nolint:errcheck // Factory pattern returns interface; error is acceptable to ignore for default
   147→func Default() Agent {
   148→	a, _ := Get(DefaultAgentName)
   149→	return a
   150→}
   151→

    12→func newHooksCmd() *cobra.Command {
    13→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    14→		Use:    "hooks",
    15→		Short:  "Hook handlers",
    16→		Long:   "Commands called by hooks. These are internal and not for direct user use.",
    17→		Hidden: true, // Internal command, not for direct user use
    18→	}
    19→
    20→	// Git hooks are strategy-level (not agent-specific)
    21→	cmd.AddCommand(newHooksGitCmd())
    22→
    23→	// Dynamically add agent hook subcommands
    24→	// Each agent that implements HookHandler gets its own subcommand tree
    25→	for _, agentName := range agent.List() {
    26→		ag, err := agent.Get(agentName)
    27→		if err != nil {
    28→			continue
    29→		}
    30→		if handler, ok := ag.(agent.HookHandler); ok {
    31→			cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHooksCmd(agentName, handler))
    32→		}
    33→	}
    34→
    35→	return cmd
    36→}

     1→// hook_registry.go provides hook command registration for agents.
     2→// The lifecycle dispatcher (DispatchLifecycleEvent) handles all lifecycle events.
     3→// PostTodo is the only hook that's handled directly (not via lifecycle dispatcher).
     4→package cli
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"context"
     8→	"errors"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"log/slog"
    11→	"time"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/geminicli"
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    18→
    19→	"github.com/spf13/cobra"
    20→)
    21→
    22→// agentHookLogCleanup stores the cleanup function for agent hook logging.
    23→// Set by PersistentPreRunE, called by PersistentPostRunE.
    24→var agentHookLogCleanup func()
    25→
    26→// currentHookAgentName stores the agent name for the currently executing hook.
    27→// Set by newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging before calling the handler.
    28→// This allows handlers to know which agent invoked the hook without guessing.
    29→var currentHookAgentName agent.AgentName
    30→
    31→// GetCurrentHookAgent returns the agent for the currently executing hook.
    32→// Returns the agent based on the hook command structure (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→// rather than guessing from directory presence.
    34→// Falls back to GetAgent() if not in a hook context.
    35→func GetCurrentHookAgent() (agent.Agent, error) {
    36→	if currentHookAgentName == "" {
    37→		return nil, errors.New("not in a hook context: agent name not set")
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	ag, err := agent.Get(currentHookAgentName)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("getting hook agent %q: %w", currentHookAgentName, err)
    43→	}
    44→	return ag, nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// newAgentHooksCmd creates a hooks subcommand for an agent that implements HookHandler.
    48→// It dynamically creates subcommands for each hook the agent supports.
    49→func newAgentHooksCmd(agentName agent.AgentName, handler agent.HookHandler) *cobra.Command {
    50→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    51→		Use:    string(agentName),
    52→		Short:  handler.Description() + " hook handlers",
    53→		Hidden: true,
    54→		PersistentPreRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    55→			agentHookLogCleanup = initHookLogging()
    56→			return nil
    57→		},
    58→		PersistentPostRunE: func(_ *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    59→			if agentHookLogCleanup != nil {
    60→				agentHookLogCleanup()
    61→			}
    62→			return nil
    63→		},
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	for _, hookName := range handler.GetHookNames() {
    67→		cmd.AddCommand(newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName, hookName))
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	return cmd
    71→}
    72→
    73→// getHookType returns the hook type based on the hook name.
    74→// Returns "subagent" for task-related hooks (pre-task, post-task, post-todo),
    75→// "tool" for tool-related hooks (before-tool, after-tool),
    76→// "agent" for all other agent hooks.
    77→func getHookType(hookName string) string {
    78→	switch hookName {
    79→	case claudecode.HookNamePreTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTask, claudecode.HookNamePostTodo:
    80→		return "subagent"
    81→	case geminicli.HookNameBeforeTool, geminicli.HookNameAfterTool:
    82→		return "tool"
    83→	default:
    84→		return "agent"
    85→	}
    86→}
    87→
    88→// newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging creates a command for a specific hook verb with structured logging.
    89→// It uses the lifecycle dispatcher (ParseHookEvent → DispatchLifecycleEvent) as the primary path.
    90→// PostTodo is handled directly as it's Claude-specific and not part of the lifecycle dispatcher.
    91→func newAgentHookVerbCmdWithLogging(agentName agent.AgentName, hookName string) *cobra.Command {
    92→	return &cobra.Command{
    93→		Use:    hookName,
    94→		Hidden: true,
    95→		Short:  "Called on " + hookName,
    96→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    97→			// Skip silently if not in a git repository - hooks shouldn't prevent the agent from working
    98→			if _, err := paths.RepoRoot(); err != nil {
    99→				return nil
   100→			}
   101→
   102→			// Skip if Entire is not enabled
   103→			enabled, err := IsEnabled()
   104→			if err == nil && !enabled {
   105→				return nil
   106→			}
   107→
   108→			start := time.Now()
   109→
   110→			// Initialize logging context with agent name
   111→			ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), agentName)
   112→
   113→			// Get strategy name for logging
   114→			strategyName := unknownStrategyName //nolint:ineffassign,wastedassign // already present in codebase
   115→			strategyName = GetStrategy().Name()
   116→
   117→			hookType := getHookType(hookName)
   118→
   119→			logging.Debug(ctx, "hook invoked",
   120→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   121→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   122→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   123→			)
   124→
   125→			// Set the current hook agent so handlers can retrieve it
   126→			currentHookAgentName = agentName
   127→			defer func() { currentHookAgentName = "" }()
   128→
   129→			// Use the lifecycle dispatcher for all hooks
   130→			var hookErr error
   131→			ag, agentErr := agent.Get(agentName)
   132→			if agentErr != nil {
   133→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent %q: %w", agentName, agentErr)
   134→			}
   135→
   136→			// Use cmd.InOrStdin() to support testing with cmd.SetIn()
   137→			event, parseErr := ag.ParseHookEvent(hookName, cmd.InOrStdin())
   138→			if parseErr != nil {
   139→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook event: %w", parseErr)
   140→			}
   141→
   142→			if event != nil {
   143→				// Lifecycle event — use the generic dispatcher
   144→				hookErr = DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag, event)
   145→			} else if agentName == agent.AgentNameClaudeCode && hookName == claudecode.HookNamePostTodo {
   146→				// PostTodo is Claude-specific: creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution
   147→				hookErr = handleClaudeCodePostTodo()
   148→			}
   149→			// Other pass-through hooks (nil event, no special handling) are no-ops
   150→
   151→			logging.LogDuration(ctx, slog.LevelDebug, "hook completed", start,
   152→				slog.String("hook", hookName),
   153→				slog.String("hook_type", hookType),
   154→				slog.String("strategy", strategyName),
   155→				slog.Bool("success", hookErr == nil),
   156→			)
   157→
   158→			return hookErr
   159→		},
   160→	}
   161→}
   162→

    22→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameClaudeCode, NewClaudeCodeAgent)

    24→
    25→// ClaudeCodeAgent implements the Agent interface for Claude Code.
    26→//
    27→//nolint:revive // ClaudeCodeAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    28→type ClaudeCodeAgent struct{}
    29→
    30→// NewClaudeCodeAgent creates a new Claude Code agent instance.
    31→func NewClaudeCodeAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    37→	return agent.AgentNameClaudeCode
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.
    41→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {
    42→	return agent.AgentTypeClaudeCode
    43→}
    44→
    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    46→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) Description() string {
    47→	return "Claude Code - Anthropic's CLI coding assistant"
    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    51→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .claude directory
    61→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Claude's hook config file.
    74→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".claude/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Claude Code supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    84→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   103→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   110→
   111→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   112→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   113→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   114→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   115→		}
   116→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   117→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   120→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   121→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   122→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   123→		}
   124→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   125→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   126→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   127→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   128→
   129→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   130→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   131→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   132→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   133→		}
   134→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   135→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   136→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   137→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   138→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   139→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   140→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   141→		}
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	return input, nil
   145→}
   146→
   147→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   148→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   149→	return input.SessionID
   150→}
   151→
   152→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Claude session file.
   153→// Claude names files directly as <id>.jsonl.
   154→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   155→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   156→}
   157→
   158→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Claude uses for config/state.
   159→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".claude"} }
   160→
   161→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   162→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   163→	// Check for test environment override
   164→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   165→		return override, nil
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   174→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   178→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   179→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   180→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   181→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   182→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   186→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   192→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   198→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   199→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   200→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   201→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   202→		NativeData:    data,
   203→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   204→	}, nil
   205→}
   206→
   207→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   208→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   209→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   210→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   211→	if session == nil {
   212→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   216→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   217→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   218→	}
   219→
   220→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   221→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   225→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   226→	}
   227→
   228→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   229→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   230→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	return nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   237→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   238→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   239→}
   240→
   241→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   242→
   243→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   244→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   245→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   246→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   247→		return ""
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return ""
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   256→}
   257→
   258→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   259→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   260→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   261→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   262→	if session == nil {
   263→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   264→	}
   265→
   266→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   267→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	if uuid == "" {
   271→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   272→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   273→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   274→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   275→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   276→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   277→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   278→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   279→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   280→		}, nil
   281→	}
   282→
   283→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   284→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   290→
   291→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   297→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   298→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   299→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   300→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   301→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   302→		NativeData:    newData,
   303→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   304→	}, nil
   305→}
   306→
   307→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   308→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   309→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   310→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   311→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   312→		return "", false
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(session.NativeData)
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		return "", false
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   321→}
   322→
   323→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   324→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   325→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   326→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   327→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   328→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   329→	})
   330→}
   331→
   332→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   333→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   334→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   335→
   336→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   337→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   338→}
   339→
   340→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   341→
   342→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   343→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   344→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   345→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   346→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   347→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   348→	if path == "" {
   349→		return 0, nil
   350→	}
   351→
   352→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   353→	if err != nil {
   354→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   355→			return 0, nil
   356→		}
   357→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   358→	}
   359→	defer file.Close()
   360→
   361→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   362→	lineCount := 0
   363→
   364→	for {
   365→		line, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   366→		if err != nil {
   367→			if err == io.EOF {
   368→				if len(line) > 0 {
   369→					lineCount++ // Count final line without trailing newline
   370→				}
   371→				break
   372→			}
   373→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   374→		}
   375→		lineCount++
   376→	}
   377→
   378→	return lineCount, nil
   379→}
   380→
   381→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   382→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   383→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   384→// Returns:
   385→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   386→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   387→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   388→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   389→	if path == "" {
   390→		return nil, 0, nil
   391→	}
   392→
   393→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   394→	if openErr != nil {
   395→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   396→	}
   397→	defer file.Close()
   398→
   399→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   400→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   401→	lineNum := 0
   402→
   403→	for {
   404→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   405→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   406→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   407→		}
   408→
   409→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   410→			lineNum++
   411→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   412→				var line TranscriptLine
   413→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   414→					lines = append(lines, line)
   415→				}
   416→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   417→			}
   418→		}
   419→
   420→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   421→			break
   422→		}
   423→	}
   424→
   425→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   426→}
   427→
   428→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   429→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   430→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   431→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   432→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   433→	if err != nil {
   434→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   435→	}
   436→	return chunks, nil
   437→}
   438→
   439→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   440→//
   441→
   442→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   443→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// SubagentAwareExtractor interface implementation
   447→
   448→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   449→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   450→// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   451→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   452→	return ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   453→}
   454→
   455→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   456→// Claude Code spawns subagents via the Task tool; their transcripts are stored in subagentsDir.
   457→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   458→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, subagentsDir)
   459→}
   460→

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import "encoding/json"
     4→
     5→// ClaudeSettings represents the .claude/settings.json structure
     6→type ClaudeSettings struct {
     7→	Hooks ClaudeHooks `json:"hooks"`
     8→}
     9→
    10→// ClaudeHooks contains the hook configurations
    11→type ClaudeHooks struct {
    12→	SessionStart     []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    13→	SessionEnd       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    14→	UserPromptSubmit []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"UserPromptSubmit,omitempty"`
    15→	Stop             []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"Stop,omitempty"`
    16→	PreToolUse       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PreToolUse,omitempty"`
    17→	PostToolUse      []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PostToolUse,omitempty"`
    18→}
    19→
    20→// ClaudeHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    21→type ClaudeHookMatcher struct {
    22→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher"`
    23→	Hooks   []ClaudeHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    24→}
    25→
    26→// ClaudeHookEntry represents a single hook command
    27→type ClaudeHookEntry struct {
    28→	Type    string `json:"type"`
    29→	Command string `json:"command"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd/Stop hooks
    33→type sessionInfoRaw struct {
    34→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    35→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// userPromptSubmitRaw is the JSON structure from UserPromptSubmit hooks.
    39→// Unlike other session hooks, this includes the user's prompt text.
    40→type userPromptSubmitRaw struct {
    41→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
    42→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    43→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt"`
    44→}
    45→
    46→// taskHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PreToolUse[Task] hook
    47→type taskHookInputRaw struct {
    48→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    49→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    50→	ToolUseID      string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
    51→	ToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:"tool_input"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// postToolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PostToolUse hooks
    55→type postToolHookInputRaw struct {
    56→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    57→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    58→	ToolUseID      string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
    59→	ToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:"tool_input"`
    60→	ToolResponse   struct {
    61→		AgentID string `json:"agentId"`
    62→	} `json:"tool_response"`
    63→}
    64→
    65→// Tool names used in Claude Code transcripts
    66→const (
    67→	ToolWrite        = "Write"
    68→	ToolEdit         = "Edit"
    69→	ToolNotebookEdit = "NotebookEdit"
    70→	ToolMCPWrite     = "mcp__acp__Write" //nolint:gosec // G101: This is a tool name, not a credential
    71→	ToolMCPEdit      = "mcp__acp__Edit"
    72→)
    73→
    74→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files
    75→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    76→	ToolWrite,
    77→	ToolEdit,
    78→	ToolNotebookEdit,
    79→	ToolMCPWrite,
    80→	ToolMCPEdit,
    81→}
    82→
    83→// messageUsage represents token usage from a Claude API response.

    85→type messageUsage struct {
    86→	InputTokens              int `json:"input_tokens"`
    87→	CacheCreationInputTokens int `json:"cache_creation_input_tokens"`
    88→	CacheReadInputTokens     int `json:"cache_read_input_tokens"`
    89→	OutputTokens             int `json:"output_tokens"`

    92→// messageWithUsage represents an assistant message with usage data.

    97→}

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"context"
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"errors"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"log/slog"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"strings"
    12→	"time"
    13→
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    15→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"

    18→)
    19→
    20→// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.
    21→var (
    22→	_ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer     = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    23→	_ agent.TranscriptPreparer     = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    24→	_ agent.TokenCalculator        = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    25→	_ agent.SubagentAwareExtractor = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    26→)
    27→
    28→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    29→// This is the new Agent interface method; delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    30→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) HookNames() []string {
    31→	return c.GetHookNames()
    32→}
    33→
    34→// ParseHookEvent translates a Claude Code hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    35→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    36→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    37→	switch hookName {
    38→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    39→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    40→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    41→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    42→	case HookNameStop:
    43→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    44→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    45→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    46→	case HookNamePreTask:
    47→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    48→	case HookNamePostTask:
    49→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    50→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    51→		// PostTodo is Claude-specific; handled outside the generic dispatcher.
    52→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    53→	default:
    54→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    55→	}
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    59→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    60→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→	return data, nil
    65→}
    66→
    67→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    68→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    69→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	var prompts []string
    75→	for i := range lines {
    76→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    77→			continue
    78→		}
    79→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    80→		if content != "" {
    81→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    82→		}
    83→	}
    84→	return prompts, nil
    85→}
    86→
    87→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    88→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    89→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    90→	if err != nil {
    91→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    95→	if parseErr != nil {
    96→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Walk backward to find last assistant text block
   100→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   101→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   102→			continue
   103→		}
   104→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   105→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   106→			continue
   107→		}
   108→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   109→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   110→				return block.Text, nil
   111→			}
   112→		}
   113→	}
   114→	return "", nil
   115→}
   116→
   117→// PrepareTranscript waits for Claude Code's async transcript flush to complete.
   118→// Claude writes a hook_progress sentinel entry after flushing all pending writes.
   119→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error {
   120→	waitForTranscriptFlush(sessionRef, time.Now())
   121→	return nil
   122→}
   123→
   124→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
   125→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   126→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<sessionID>/subagents/
   127→	// but we don't have the sessionID here. The caller should pass the transcript path
   128→	// which may contain the session ID in its directory structure.
   129→	// For now, compute subagentsDir from the transcript path structure.
   130→	return CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef, fromOffset, "")
   131→}

   135→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   136→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)

   222→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   223→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   224→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→	if len(data) == 0 {
   229→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   230→	}
   231→	var result T
   232→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   233→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   234→	}
   235→	return &result, nil
   236→}
   237→
   238→// --- Transcript flush sentinel ---
   239→
   240→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   241→// entry when the stop hook has been invoked, indicating the transcript is fully flushed.
   242→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   243→
   244→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file for the stop hook sentinel.
   245→// Falls back silently after a timeout.
   246→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   247→	const (
   248→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   249→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   250→		tailBytes    = 4096
   251→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   252→	)
   253→
   254→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "agent.claudecode")
   255→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   256→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   257→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   258→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   259→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   260→			)
   261→			return
   262→		}
   263→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   264→	}
   265→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   266→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   267→	)
   268→}
   269→
   270→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for the sentinel.
   271→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   272→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   273→	if err != nil {
   274→		return false
   275→	}
   276→	defer f.Close()
   277→
   278→	info, err := f.Stat()
   279→	if err != nil {
   280→		return false
   281→	}
   282→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   283→	if offset < 0 {
   284→		offset = 0
   285→	}
   286→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   287→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   288→		return false
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   292→	for _, line := range lines {
   293→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   294→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   295→			continue
   296→		}
   297→
   298→		var entry struct {
   299→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   300→		}
   301→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   302→			continue
   303→		}
   304→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   305→		if err != nil {
   306→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   307→			if err != nil {
   308→				continue
   309→			}
   310→		}
   311→		// Validate timestamp is within acceptable range:
   312→		// - Not too far in the past (before hook started minus skew)
   313→		// - Not too far in the future (after hook started plus skew)
   314→		lowerBound := hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)
   315→		upperBound := hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)
   316→		if ts.After(lowerBound) && ts.Before(upperBound) {
   317→			return true
   318→		}
   319→	}
   320→	return false
   321→}
   322→
```

    63→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    64→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .claude directory
    65→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    66→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
