   243→
   244→// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   268→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., "Notification", "SubagentStop")
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse []ClaudeHookMatcher
   298→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   299→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   300→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", &stop)
   301→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", &userPromptSubmit)
   302→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", &preToolUse)
   303→	parseHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", &postToolUse)
   304→
   305→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   306→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   307→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   308→	stop = removeEntireHooks(stop)
   309→	userPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)
   310→	preToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)
   311→	postToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)
   312→
   313→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   314→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   315→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   316→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "Stop", stop)
   317→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "UserPromptSubmit", userPromptSubmit)
   318→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PreToolUse", preToolUse)
   319→	marshalHookType(rawHooks, "PostToolUse", postToolUse)
   320→
   321→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   322→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   323→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   324→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   325→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   326→			rawPermissions = nil
   327→		}
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   331→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   332→			var denyRules []string
   333→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   334→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   335→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   336→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   337→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   338→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   339→					}
   340→				}
   341→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   342→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   343→					if err == nil {
   344→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   345→					}
   346→				} else {
   347→					// Remove empty deny array
   348→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   349→				}
   350→			}
   351→		}
   352→
   353→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   354→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   355→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   356→			if err == nil {
   357→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   358→			}
   359→		} else {
   360→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   361→		}
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   365→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   366→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   367→		if err != nil {
   368→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   369→		}
   370→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   371→	} else {
   372→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Write back
   376→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   377→	if err != nil {
   378→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   379→	}
   380→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→	return nil
   384→}
   385→
   386→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   387→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   388→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   389→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   390→	if err != nil {
   391→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   392→	}
   393→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   394→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   395→	if err != nil {
   396→		return false
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   400→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   401→		return false
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   405→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   406→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   407→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   408→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   409→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   410→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   411→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   412→}
   413→
   414→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   415→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   416→	return []agent.HookType{
   417→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   418→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   419→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   420→		agent.HookStop,
   421→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   422→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   423→	}
   424→}
   425→
   426→// Helper functions for hook management
   427→
   428→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   431→			if hook.Command == command {
   432→				return true
   433→			}
   434→		}
   435→	}
   436→	return false
   437→}
   438→
   439→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   440→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   441→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   442→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   443→				if hook.Command == command {
   444→					return true
   445→				}
   446→			}
   447→		}
   448→	}
   449→	return false
   450→}
   451→
   452→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   453→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   454→		Type:    "command",
   455→		Command: command,
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   459→	if matcherName == "" {
   460→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   461→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   462→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   463→				return matchers
   464→			}
   465→		}
   466→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   467→			Matcher: "",
   468→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   469→		})
   470→	}
   471→
   472→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   473→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   474→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   475→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   476→			return matchers
   477→		}
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   481→		Matcher: matcherName,
   482→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   483→	})
   484→}
   485→
   486→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   487→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   488→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   489→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   490→			return true
   491→		}
   492→	}
   493→	return false
   494→}
   495→
   496→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   497→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   498→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   499→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   500→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   501→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   502→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   503→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   504→			}
   505→		}
   506→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   507→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   508→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   509→			result = append(result, matcher)
   510→		}
   511→	}
   512→	return result
   513→}
   514→
   515→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   516→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   517→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   518→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   519→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   520→}
   521→

   648→// validateSetupFlags checks that --local and --project flags are not both specified.
   649→func validateSetupFlags(useLocal, useProject bool) error {
   650→	if useLocal && useProject {
   651→		return errors.New("cannot specify both --project and --local")
   652→	}
   653→	return nil
   654→}
   655→
   656→// determineSettingsTarget decides whether to write to settings.local.json based on:
   657→// - Whether settings.json already exists
   658→// - The --local and --project flags
   659→// Returns (useLocal, showNotification).
   660→func determineSettingsTarget(entireDir string, useLocal, useProject bool) (bool, bool) {
   661→	// Explicit --local flag always uses local settings
   662→	if useLocal {
   663→		return true, false
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	// Explicit --project flag always uses project settings
   667→	if useProject {
   668→		return false, false
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	// No flags specified - check if settings file exists
   672→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(entireDir, paths.SettingsFileName)
   673→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsPath); err == nil {
   674→		// Settings file exists - auto-redirect to local with notification
   675→		return true, true
   676→	}
   677→
   678→	// Settings file doesn't exist - create it
   679→	return false, false

    62→// InstallHooks installs Gemini CLI hooks in .gemini/settings.json.
    63→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    64→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    65→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    66→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .gemini directory
    67→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory

    81→
    82→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types

   108→	}
   109→
   110→	// Enable hooks via hooksConfig
   111→	// hooksConfig.Enabled must be true for Gemini CLI to execute hooks
   112→	hooksConfig.Enabled = true
   113→
   114→	// Define hook commands based on localDev mode
   115→	var cmdPrefix string
   116→	if localDev {
   117→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks gemini "
   118→	} else {
   119→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks gemini "
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   123→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, beforeAgent, afterAgent []GeminiHookMatcher
   124→	var beforeModel, afterModel, beforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher
   125→	var beforeTool, afterTool, preCompress, notification []GeminiHookMatcher
   126→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   127→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   128→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", &beforeAgent)

   131→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", &afterModel)

   133→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", &beforeTool)
   134→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", &afterTool)
   135→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", &preCompress)
   136→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", &notification)
   137→
   138→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
   139→	// If the exact same hook command already exists, return 0 (no changes needed)
   140→	if !force {
   141→		existingCmd := getFirstEntireHookCommand(sessionStart)
   142→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + "session-start"
   143→		if existingCmd == expectedCmd {
   144→			return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   145→		}
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first (for clean installs and mode switching)
   149→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   150→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)

   152→	afterAgent = removeEntireHooks(afterAgent)

   154→	afterModel = removeEntireHooks(afterModel)

   158→	preCompress = removeEntireHooks(preCompress)
   159→	notification = removeEntireHooks(notification)
   160→
   161→	// Install all hooks
   162→	// Session lifecycle hooks
   163→	sessionStart = addGeminiHook(sessionStart, "", "entire-session-start", cmdPrefix+"session-start")
   164→	// SessionEnd fires on both "exit" and "logout" - install hooks for both matchers
   165→	sessionEnd = addGeminiHook(sessionEnd, "exit", "entire-session-end-exit", cmdPrefix+"session-end")
   166→	sessionEnd = addGeminiHook(sessionEnd, "logout", "entire-session-end-logout", cmdPrefix+"session-end")
   167→
   168→	// Agent hooks (user prompt and response)
   169→	beforeAgent = addGeminiHook(beforeAgent, "", "entire-before-agent", cmdPrefix+"before-agent")
   170→	afterAgent = addGeminiHook(afterAgent, "", "entire-after-agent", cmdPrefix+"after-agent")
   171→
   172→	// Model hooks (LLM request/response - fires on every LLM call)
   173→	beforeModel = addGeminiHook(beforeModel, "", "entire-before-model", cmdPrefix+"before-model")
   174→	afterModel = addGeminiHook(afterModel, "", "entire-after-model", cmdPrefix+"after-model")
   175→
   176→	// Tool selection hook (before planner selects tools)
   177→	beforeToolSelection = addGeminiHook(beforeToolSelection, "", "entire-before-tool-selection", cmdPrefix+"before-tool-selection")
   178→
   179→	// Tool hooks (before/after tool execution)
   180→	beforeTool = addGeminiHook(beforeTool, "*", "entire-before-tool", cmdPrefix+"before-tool")
   181→	afterTool = addGeminiHook(afterTool, "*", "entire-after-tool", cmdPrefix+"after-tool")
   182→
   183→	// Compression hook (before chat history compression)
   184→	preCompress = addGeminiHook(preCompress, "", "entire-pre-compress", cmdPrefix+"pre-compress")
   185→
   186→	// Notification hook (errors, warnings, info)
   187→	notification = addGeminiHook(notification, "", "entire-notification", cmdPrefix+"notification")
   188→
   189→	// 12 hooks total:
   190→	// - session-start (1)
   191→	// - session-end exit + logout (2)
   192→	// - before-agent, after-agent (2)
   193→	// - before-model, after-model (2)
   194→	// - before-tool-selection (1)
   195→	// - before-tool, after-tool (2)
   196→	// - pre-compress (1)
   197→	// - notification (1)
   198→	count := 12
   199→
   200→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   201→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   202→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   203→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", beforeAgent)
   204→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", afterAgent)
   205→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", beforeModel)
   206→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", afterModel)
   207→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", beforeToolSelection)
   208→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", beforeTool)
   209→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", afterTool)
   210→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", preCompress)
   211→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", notification)
   212→
   213→	// Marshal hooksConfig back to raw settings
   214→	hooksConfigJSON, err := json.Marshal(hooksConfig)
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooksConfig: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→	rawSettings["hooksConfig"] = hooksConfigJSON
   219→
   220→	// Marshal hooks back to raw settings (preserving unknown hook types)
   221→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   226→
   227→	// Write back to file
   228→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   229→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .gemini directory: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   233→	if err != nil {
   234→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   235→	}
   236→
   237→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   238→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	return count, nil
   242→}
   243→
   244→// parseGeminiHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.
   245→// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).
   246→func parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]GeminiHookMatcher) {
   247→	if data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {
   248→		//nolint:errcheck,gosec // Intentionally ignoring parse errors - leave target as nil/empty
   249→		json.Unmarshal(data, target)
   250→	}
   251→}
   252→
   253→// marshalGeminiHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.
   254→// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.
   255→func marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) {
   256→	if len(matchers) == 0 {
   257→		delete(rawHooks, hookType)
   258→		return
   259→	}
   260→	data, err := json.Marshal(matchers)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)
   263→	}
   264→	rawHooks[hookType] = data
   265→}
   266→
   267→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Gemini CLI settings.
   268→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   269→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   270→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   271→	if err != nil {
   272→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   273→	}
   274→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   275→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   276→	if err != nil {
   277→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   278→	}
   279→
   280→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   281→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   282→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types
   286→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
   287→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   288→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
   289→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   290→		}
   291→	}
   292→	if rawHooks == nil {
   293→		rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Parse only the hook types we need to modify
   297→	var sessionStart, sessionEnd, beforeAgent, afterAgent []GeminiHookMatcher
   298→	var beforeModel, afterModel, beforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher
   299→	var beforeTool, afterTool, preCompress, notification []GeminiHookMatcher
   300→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", &sessionStart)
   301→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", &sessionEnd)
   302→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", &beforeAgent)
   303→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", &afterAgent)
   304→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", &beforeModel)
   305→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", &afterModel)
   306→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", &beforeToolSelection)
   307→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", &beforeTool)
   308→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", &afterTool)
   309→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", &preCompress)
   310→	parseGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", &notification)
   311→
   312→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   313→	sessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)
   314→	sessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)
   315→	beforeAgent = removeEntireHooks(beforeAgent)
   316→	afterAgent = removeEntireHooks(afterAgent)
   317→	beforeModel = removeEntireHooks(beforeModel)
   318→	afterModel = removeEntireHooks(afterModel)
   319→	beforeToolSelection = removeEntireHooks(beforeToolSelection)
   320→	beforeTool = removeEntireHooks(beforeTool)
   321→	afterTool = removeEntireHooks(afterTool)
   322→	preCompress = removeEntireHooks(preCompress)
   323→	notification = removeEntireHooks(notification)
   324→
   325→	// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks
   326→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionStart", sessionStart)
   327→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "SessionEnd", sessionEnd)
   328→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeAgent", beforeAgent)
   329→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterAgent", afterAgent)
   330→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeModel", beforeModel)
   331→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterModel", afterModel)
   332→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeToolSelection", beforeToolSelection)
   333→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "BeforeTool", beforeTool)
   334→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "AfterTool", afterTool)
   335→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "PreCompress", preCompress)
   336→	marshalGeminiHookType(rawHooks, "Notification", notification)
   337→
   338→	// Marshal hooks back (preserving unknown hook types)
   339→	if len(rawHooks) > 0 {
   340→		hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)
   341→		if err != nil {
   342→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   343→		}
   344→		rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   345→	} else {
   346→		delete(rawSettings, "hooks")
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	// Write back
   350→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   351→	if err != nil {
   352→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   356→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→	return nil
   359→}
   360→
   361→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   362→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   363→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   364→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   367→	}
   368→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   369→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   370→	if err != nil {
   371→		return false
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	var settings GeminiSettings
   375→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   376→		return false
   377→	}
   378→
   379→	// Check for at least one of our hooks using isEntireHook (works for both localDev and production)
   380→	return hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionStart) ||
   381→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd) ||
   382→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) ||
   383→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) ||
   384→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel) ||
   385→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterModel) ||
   386→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection) ||
   387→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool) ||
   388→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterTool) ||
   389→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.PreCompress) ||
   390→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   391→}
   392→
   393→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Gemini CLI supports.
   394→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   395→	return []agent.HookType{
   396→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   397→		agent.HookSessionEnd,       // Maps to Gemini's SessionEnd (explicit exit/logout)
   398→		agent.HookStop,             // Maps to Gemini's AfterAgent (end of response)
   399→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, // Maps to Gemini's BeforeAgent (user prompt)
   400→		agent.HookPreToolUse,       // Maps to Gemini's BeforeTool
   401→		agent.HookPostToolUse,      // Maps to Gemini's AfterTool
   402→	}
   403→}
   404→
   405→// Helper functions for hook management
   406→
   407→// addGeminiHook adds a hook entry to matchers.
   408→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini hooks require a "name" field.
   409→func addGeminiHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher, matcherName, hookName, command string) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   410→	entry := GeminiHookEntry{
   411→		Name:    hookName,
   412→		Type:    "command",
   413→		Command: command,
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Find or create matcher
   417→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   418→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   419→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   420→			return matchers
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Create new matcher
   425→	newMatcher := GeminiHookMatcher{
   426→		Hooks: []GeminiHookEntry{entry},
   427→	}
   428→	if matcherName != "" {
   429→		newMatcher.Matcher = matcherName
   430→	}
   431→	return append(matchers, newMatcher)
   432→}
   433→
   434→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   435→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   436→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   437→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   438→			return true
   439→		}
   440→	}
   441→	return false
   442→}
   443→
   444→// hasEntireHook checks if any hook in the matchers is an Entire hook
   445→func hasEntireHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) bool {
   446→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   447→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   448→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   449→				return true
   450→			}
   451→		}
   452→	}
   453→	return false
   454→}
   455→
   456→// getFirstEntireHookCommand returns the command of the first Entire hook found, or empty string
   457→func getFirstEntireHookCommand(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) string {
   458→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   459→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   460→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   461→				return hook.Command
   462→			}
   463→		}
   464→	}
   465→	return ""
   466→}
   467→
   468→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers
   469→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   470→	result := make([]GeminiHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   471→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   472→		filteredHooks := make([]GeminiHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   473→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   474→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   475→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   476→			}
   477→		}
   478→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   479→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   480→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   481→			result = append(result, matcher)
   482→		}
   483→	}
   484→	return result
   485→}
   486→
```

     1→// hooks_claudecode_posttodo.go contains the PostTodo hook handler for Claude Code.
     2→// This is a Claude-specific hook that creates incremental checkpoints during subagent execution.
     3→// It's not part of the generic lifecycle dispatcher because it requires special handling:
     4→// - Only fires for TodoWrite tool invocations
     5→// - Creates incremental checkpoints (not full checkpoints)
     6→// - Only activates when in subagent context (pre-task file exists)
     7→package cli
     8→
     9→import (
    10→	"context"
    11→	"fmt"
    12→	"io"
    13→	"log/slog"
    14→	"os"
    15→
    16→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    17→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
    18→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    19→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/strategy"
    20→)
    21→
    22→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
    23→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
    24→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
    25→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
    26→	return handleClaudeCodePostTodoFromReader(os.Stdin)
    27→}
    28→
    29→// handleClaudeCodePostTodoFromReader is the testable version that accepts an io.Reader.
    30→func handleClaudeCodePostTodoFromReader(reader io.Reader) error {
    31→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(reader)
    32→	if err != nil {
    33→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
    34→	}
    35→
    36→	// Get agent for logging context
    37→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    38→	if err != nil {
    39→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    40→	}
    41→
    42→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    43→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
    44→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
    45→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
    46→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    47→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
    48→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
    49→	)
    50→
    51→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
    52→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
    53→	if !found {
    54→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
    55→		return nil
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
    59→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
    60→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
    61→		return nil
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
    65→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
    68→		return nil
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
    72→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
    73→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
    74→		return nil
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	// Get git author
    78→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
    79→	if err != nil {
    80→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
    81→		return nil
    82→	}
    83→
    84→	// Get the active strategy
    85→	strat := GetStrategy()
    86→
    87→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
    88→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    89→	if sessionID == "" {
    90→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
    94→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
    95→
    96→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
    97→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
    98→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
    99→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   100→	if todoContent == "" {
   101→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   102→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   103→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   104→		if todoCount > 0 {
   105→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   106→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   107→		}
   108→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   109→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   113→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   114→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   115→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   116→	}
   117→
   118→	// Build incremental task step context
   119→	ctx := strategy.TaskStepContext{
   120→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   121→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   122→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   123→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   124→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   125→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   126→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   127→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   128→		IsIncremental:       true,
   129→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   130→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   131→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   132→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   133→		AgentType:           agentType,
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	// Save incremental task step
   137→	if err := strat.SaveTaskStep(ctx); err != nil {
   138→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental task step: %v\n", err)
   139→		return nil
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   143→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   144→	return nil
   145→}
   146→

     5→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure
     6→type GeminiSettings struct {
     7→	HooksConfig GeminiHooksConfig `json:"hooksConfig,omitempty"`
     8→	Hooks       GeminiHooks       `json:"hooks,omitempty"`
     9→}
    10→
    11→// GeminiHooksConfig contains tool-related settings
    12→type GeminiHooksConfig struct {

    15→
    16→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations
    17→type GeminiHooks struct {
    18→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    19→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    20→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    24→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    25→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    26→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    27→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    28→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    29→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns
    33→type GeminiHookMatcher struct {
    34→	Matcher string            `json:"matcher,omitempty"`
    35→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`
    36→}
    37→
    38→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.

    40→type GeminiHookEntry struct {

    59→type agentHookInputRaw struct {

    61→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path"`
    62→	Cwd            string `json:"cwd"`
    63→	HookEventName  string `json:"hook_event_name"`
    64→	Timestamp      string `json:"timestamp"`
    65→	Prompt         string `json:"prompt,omitempty"` // User's prompt (BeforeAgent only)
    66→}
    67→
    68→// toolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeTool/AfterTool hooks
    69→type toolHookInputRaw struct {
    70→	SessionID      string          `json:"session_id"`
    71→	TranscriptPath string          `json:"transcript_path"`
    72→	Cwd            string          `json:"cwd"`
    73→	HookEventName  string          `json:"hook_event_name"`
    74→	Timestamp      string          `json:"timestamp"`
    75→	ToolName       string          `json:"tool_name"`
    76→	ToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:"tool_input"`
    77→	ToolResponse   json.RawMessage `json:"tool_response,omitempty"` // Only for AfterTool
    78→}
    79→
    80→// Tool names used in Gemini CLI that modify files
    81→// Note: Gemini CLI uses different names in different contexts:
    82→// - Internal/transcript names: write_file, replace
    83→// - Display names: WriteFile, Edit
    84→const (
    85→	ToolWriteFile = "write_file"
    86→	ToolEditFile  = "edit_file"
    87→	ToolSaveFile  = "save_file"
    88→	ToolReplace   = "replace"
    89→)
    90→
    91→// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files in Gemini CLI
    92→var FileModificationTools = []string{
    93→	ToolWriteFile,
    94→	ToolEditFile,
    95→	ToolSaveFile,
    96→	ToolReplace,
    97→}
    98→
    99→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.
   100→// This is specific to Gemini's API format where each message has a tokens object.
   101→type geminiMessageTokens struct {
   102→	Input    int `json:"input"`
   103→	Output   int `json:"output"`
   104→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
   105→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
   106→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`
   107→	Total    int `json:"total"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.
   111→// Used for extracting token counts from Gemini transcripts.
   112→type geminiMessageWithTokens struct {
   113→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   114→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   115→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   116→}
   117→

    14→// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.

    17→	_ agent.TokenCalculator    = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)

    20→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Gemini CLI supports.
    21→// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    22→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) HookNames() []string {
    23→	return g.GetHookNames()
    24→}
    25→
    26→// ParseHookEvent translates a Gemini CLI hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    27→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    28→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    29→	switch hookName {
    30→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    31→		return g.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    32→	case HookNameBeforeAgent:
    33→		return g.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    34→	case HookNameAfterAgent:
    35→		return g.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    36→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    37→		return g.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    38→	case HookNamePreCompress:
    39→		return g.parseCompaction(stdin)
    40→	case HookNameBeforeTool, HookNameAfterTool, HookNameBeforeModel,
    41→		HookNameAfterModel, HookNameBeforeToolSelection, HookNameNotification:
    42→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    44→	default:
    45→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSON transcript bytes for a session.
    50→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	return data, nil
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    59→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    60→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	t, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
    66→	if parseErr != nil {
    67→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var prompts []string
    71→	for i := fromOffset; i < len(t.Messages); i++ {
    72→		msg := t.Messages[i]
    73→		if msg.Type == MessageTypeUser && msg.Content != "" {
    74→			prompts = append(prompts, msg.Content)
    75→		}
    76→	}
    77→	return prompts, nil
    78→}
    79→
    80→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    81→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    82→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    85→	}
    86→	return ExtractLastAssistantMessage(data)
    87→}
    88→
    89→// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given message offset.
    90→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
    91→	return CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    92→}
    93→
    94→// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---
    95→
    96→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    97→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
    98→	if err != nil {
    99→		return nil, err
   100→	}
   101→	return &agent.Event{
   102→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   103→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   104→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   105→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   106→	}, nil
   107→}
   108→
   109→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   110→	raw, err := readAndParse[agentHookInputRaw](stdin)
   111→	if err != nil {
   112→		return nil, err
   113→	}
   114→	return &agent.Event{
   115→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   116→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   117→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   118→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   119→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   120→	}, nil
   121→}
   122→
   123→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   124→	raw, err := readAndParse[agentHookInputRaw](stdin)
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return nil, err
   127→	}
   128→	return &agent.Event{
   129→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   130→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   131→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   132→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   133→	}, nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   137→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   138→	if err != nil {
   139→		return nil, err
   140→	}
   141→	return &agent.Event{
   142→		Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   143→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   144→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   145→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   146→	}, nil
   147→}
   148→
   149→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) parseCompaction(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   150→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return nil, err
   153→	}
   154→	return &agent.Event{
   155→		Type:       agent.Compaction,
   156→		SessionID:  raw.SessionID,
   157→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   158→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   159→	}, nil
   160→}
   161→
   162→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   163→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   164→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→	if len(data) == 0 {
   169→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   170→	}
   171→	var result T
   172→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   173→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   174→	}
   175→	return &result, nil
   176→}
   177→

     1→// Package geminicli implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
     2→package geminicli
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"context"
     6→	"encoding/json"

    21→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern
    22→func init() {
    23→	agent.Register(agent.AgentNameGemini, NewGeminiCLIAgent)
    24→}
    25→
    26→// GeminiCLIAgent implements the Agent interface for Gemini CLI.
    27→//
    28→//nolint:revive // GeminiCLIAgent is clearer than Agent in this context
    29→type GeminiCLIAgent struct{}
    30→
    31→func NewGeminiCLIAgent() agent.Agent {
    32→	return &GeminiCLIAgent{}
    33→}
    34→
    35→// Name returns the agent registry key.
    36→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {
    37→	return agent.AgentNameGemini
    38→}
    39→
    40→// Type returns the agent type identifier.
    41→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {
    42→	return agent.AgentTypeGemini
    43→}
    44→
    45→// Description returns a human-readable description.
    46→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) Description() string {
    47→	return "Gemini CLI - Google's AI coding assistant"
    48→}
    49→
    50→// DetectPresence checks if Gemini CLI is configured in the repository.
    51→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    52→	// Get repo root to check for .gemini directory
    53→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    54→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    55→	if err != nil {
    56→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    57→		repoRoot = "."
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	// Check for .gemini directory
    61→	geminiDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini")
    62→	if _, err := os.Stat(geminiDir); err == nil {
    63→		return true, nil
    64→	}
    65→	// Check for .gemini/settings.json
    66→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", "settings.json")
    67→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    68→		return true, nil
    69→	}
    70→	return false, nil
    71→}
    72→
    73→// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Gemini's hook config file.
    74→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {
    75→	return ".gemini/settings.json"
    76→}
    77→
    78→// SupportsHooks returns true as Gemini CLI supports lifecycle hooks.
    79→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {
    80→	return true
    81→}
    82→
    83→// ParseHookInput parses Gemini CLI hook input from stdin.
    84→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    85→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    86→	if err != nil {
    87→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	if len(data) == 0 {
    91→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    95→		HookType:  hookType,
    96→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    97→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    98→	}
    99→
   100→	// Parse based on hook type
   101→	switch hookType {
   102→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   103→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   104→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   105→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   108→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   109→		// Store Gemini-specific fields in RawData
   110→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   111→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   112→		if raw.Source != "" {
   113→			input.RawData["source"] = raw.Source
   114→		}
   115→		if raw.Reason != "" {
   116→			input.RawData["reason"] = raw.Reason
   117→		}
   118→
   119→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   120→		// BeforeAgent is Gemini's equivalent to Claude's UserPromptSubmit
   121→		// It provides the user's prompt in the "prompt" field
   122→		var raw agentHookInputRaw
   123→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   124→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse agent hook input: %w", err)
   125→		}
   126→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   127→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   128→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   129→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   130→		if raw.Prompt != "" {
   131→			input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   132→			input.RawData["prompt"] = raw.Prompt
   133→		}
   134→
   135→	case agent.HookPreToolUse, agent.HookPostToolUse:
   136→		var raw toolHookInputRaw
   137→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   138→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse tool hook input: %w", err)
   139→		}
   140→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   141→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   142→		input.ToolName = raw.ToolName
   143→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   144→		if hookType == agent.HookPostToolUse {
   145→			input.ToolResponse = raw.ToolResponse
   146→		}
   147→		input.RawData["cwd"] = raw.Cwd
   148→		input.RawData["hook_event_name"] = raw.HookEventName
   149→	}
   150→
   151→	return input, nil
   152→}
   153→
   154→// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.
   155→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {
   156→	return input.SessionID
   157→}
   158→
   159→// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Gemini uses for config/state.
   160→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{".gemini"} }
   161→
   162→// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Gemini session file.
   163→// Gemini names files as session-<date>-<shortid>.json where shortid is the first 8 chars
   164→// of the session UUID. This searches for an existing file matching the pattern, falling
   165→// back to <dir>/<id>.json if no match is found.
   166→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {
   167→	// Try to find existing file matching Gemini's naming convention:
   168→	// session-*-<first8chars>.json
   169→	shortID := agentSessionID
   170→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   171→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	pattern := filepath.Join(sessionDir, "session-*-"+shortID+".json")
   175→	matches, err := filepath.Glob(pattern)
   176→	if err == nil && len(matches) > 0 {
   177→		// Return the most recent match (last alphabetically, since date is in the name)
   178→		return matches[len(matches)-1]
   179→	}
   180→
   181→	// Fallback: construct a default path
   182→	return filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".json")
   183→}
   184→
   185→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Gemini stores session transcripts.
   186→// Gemini stores sessions in ~/.gemini/tmp/<project-hash>/chats/
   187→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   188→	// Check for test environment override
   189→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   190→		return override, nil
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   194→	if err != nil {
   195→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Gemini uses a hash of the project path for the directory name
   199→	projectDir := SanitizePathForGemini(repoPath)
   200→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".gemini", "tmp", projectDir, "chats"), nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// ReadSession reads a session from Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   204→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSON bytes.
   205→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   206→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   207→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Read the raw JSON file
   211→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   212→	if err != nil {
   213→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   214→	}
   215→
   216→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   217→	modifiedFiles, err := ExtractModifiedFiles(data)
   218→	if err != nil {
   219→		// Non-fatal: we can still return the session without modified files
   220→		modifiedFiles = nil
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   224→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   225→		AgentName:     g.Name(),
   226→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   227→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   228→		NativeData:    data,
   229→		ModifiedFiles: modifiedFiles,
   230→	}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// WriteSession writes a session to Gemini's storage (JSON transcript file).
   234→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSON bytes.
   235→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   236→	if session == nil {
   237→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Verify this session belongs to Gemini CLI
   241→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != g.Name() {
   242→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, g.Name())
   243→	}
   244→
   245→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   246→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   250→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Write the raw JSON data
   254→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	return nil
   259→}
   260→
   261→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Gemini CLI session.
   262→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   263→	return "gemini --resume " + sessionID
   264→}
   265→
   266→// SanitizePathForGemini converts a path to Gemini's project directory format.
   267→// Gemini uses a hash-like sanitization similar to Claude.
   268→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   269→
   270→func SanitizePathForGemini(path string) string {
   271→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   272→}
   273→
   274→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   275→
   276→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current message count of a Gemini transcript.
   277→// Gemini uses JSON format with a messages array, so position is the message count.
   278→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   279→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   280→	if path == "" {
   281→		return 0, nil
   282→	}
   283→
   284→	data, err := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   287→			return 0, nil
   288→		}
   289→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	if len(data) == 0 {
   293→		return 0, nil
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	transcript, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   297→	if err != nil {
   298→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   299→	}
   300→
   301→	return len(transcript.Messages), nil
   302→}
   303→
   304→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given message index.
   305→// For Gemini (JSON format), offset is the starting message index.
   306→// Returns:
   307→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Gemini (from Write/Edit tools)
   308→//   - currentPosition: total number of messages in the transcript
   309→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   310→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   311→	if path == "" {
   312→		return nil, 0, nil
   313→	}
   314→
   315→	data, readErr := os.ReadFile(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   316→	if readErr != nil {
   317→		if os.IsNotExist(readErr) {
   318→			return nil, 0, nil
   319→		}
   320→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   321→	}
   322→
   323→	if len(data) == 0 {
   324→		return nil, 0, nil
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	transcript, parseErr := ParseTranscript(data)
   328→	if parseErr != nil {
   329→		return nil, 0, parseErr
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	totalMessages := len(transcript.Messages)
   333→
   334→	// Extract files from messages starting at startOffset
   335→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   336→	for i := startOffset; i < len(transcript.Messages); i++ {
   337→		msg := transcript.Messages[i]
   338→		// Only process gemini messages (assistant messages)
   339→		if msg.Type != MessageTypeGemini {
   340→			continue
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Process tool calls in this message
   344→		for _, toolCall := range msg.ToolCalls {
   345→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
   346→			isModifyTool := false
   347→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
   348→				if toolCall.Name == name {
   349→					isModifyTool = true
   350→					break
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			if !isModifyTool {
   355→				continue
   356→			}
   357→
   358→			// Extract file path from args map
   359→			var file string
   360→			if fp, ok := toolCall.Args["file_path"].(string); ok && fp != "" {
   361→				file = fp
   362→			} else if p, ok := toolCall.Args["path"].(string); ok && p != "" {
   363→				file = p
   364→			} else if fn, ok := toolCall.Args["filename"].(string); ok && fn != "" {
   365→				file = fn
   366→			}
   367→
   368→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   369→				fileSet[file] = true
   370→				files = append(files, file)
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files, totalMessages, nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→// ChunkTranscript splits a Gemini JSON transcript by distributing messages across chunks.
   379→// Gemini uses JSON format with a {"messages": [...]} structure, so chunking splits
   380→// the messages array while preserving the JSON structure in each chunk.
   381→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   382→	var transcript GeminiTranscript
   383→	if err := json.Unmarshal(content, &transcript); err != nil {
   384→		// Fall back to JSONL chunking if not valid Gemini JSON
   385→		chunks, chunkErr := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   386→		if chunkErr != nil {
   387→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk as JSONL: %w", chunkErr)
   388→		}
   389→		return chunks, nil
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	if len(transcript.Messages) == 0 {
   393→		return [][]byte{content}, nil
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	var chunks [][]byte
   397→	var currentMessages []GeminiMessage
   398→	currentSize := len(`{"messages":[]}`) // Base JSON structure size
   399→
   400→	for i, msg := range transcript.Messages {
   401→		// Marshal message to get its size
   402→		msgBytes, err := json.Marshal(msg)
   403→		if err != nil {
   404→			logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to marshal Gemini message during chunking",
   405→				slog.Int("message_index", i),
   406→				slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   407→			)
   408→			continue
   409→		}
   410→		msgSize := len(msgBytes) + 1 // +1 for comma separator
   411→
   412→		if currentSize+msgSize > maxSize && len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   413→			// Save current chunk
   414→			chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   415→			if err != nil {
   416→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal chunk: %w", err)
   417→			}
   418→			chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   419→
   420→			// Start new chunk
   421→			currentMessages = nil
   422→			currentSize = len(`{"messages":[]}`)
   423→		}
   424→
   425→		currentMessages = append(currentMessages, msg)
   426→		currentSize += msgSize
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	// Add the last chunk
   430→	if len(currentMessages) > 0 {
   431→		chunkData, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: currentMessages})
   432→		if err != nil {
   433→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal final chunk: %w", err)
   434→		}
   435→		chunks = append(chunks, chunkData)
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Ensure we created at least one chunk (could be empty if all messages failed to marshal)
   439→	if len(chunks) == 0 {
   440→		return nil, errors.New("failed to create any chunks: all messages failed to marshal")
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	return chunks, nil
   444→}
   445→
   446→// ReassembleTranscript merges Gemini JSON chunks by combining their message arrays.
   447→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   448→	var allMessages []GeminiMessage
   449→
   450→	for _, chunk := range chunks {
   451→		var transcript GeminiTranscript
   452→		if err := json.Unmarshal(chunk, &transcript); err != nil {
   453→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal chunk: %w", err)
   454→		}
   455→		allMessages = append(allMessages, transcript.Messages...)
   456→	}
   457→
   458→	result, err := json.Marshal(GeminiTranscript{Messages: allMessages})
   459→	if err != nil {
   460→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal reassembled transcript: %w", err)
   461→	}
   462→	return result, nil
   463→}
   464→
```

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→)
     9→
    10→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    11→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    12→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    13→//
    14→// The interface is organized into four groups:
    15→//
    16→//   - Identity (5 methods): Name, Type, Description, DetectPresence, ProtectedDirs
    17→//   - Event Mapping (2 methods): HookNames, ParseHookEvent
    18→//   - Transcript Storage (3 methods): ReadTranscript, ChunkTranscript, ReassembleTranscript
    19→//   - Legacy (8 methods): Will be moved to optional interfaces or removed in a future phase
    20→type Agent interface {
    21→	// --- Identity ---
    22→
    23→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    24→	Name() AgentName
    25→
    26→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    27→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    28→	Type() AgentType
    29→
    30→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    31→	Description() string
    32→
    33→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    34→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    35→
    36→	// ProtectedDirs returns repo-root-relative directories that should never be
    37→	// modified or deleted during rewind or other destructive operations.
    38→	// Examples: [".claude"] for Claude, [".gemini"] for Gemini.
    39→	ProtectedDirs() []string
    40→
    41→	// --- Event Mapping ---
    42→
    43→	// HookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
    44→	// These become subcommands under `entire hooks <agent>`.
    45→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "session-start", "session-end"]
    46→	HookNames() []string
    47→
    48→	// ParseHookEvent translates an agent-native hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    49→	// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance (e.g., pass-through hooks).
    50→	// This is the core contribution surface for new agent implementations.
    51→	ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*Event, error)
    52→
    53→	// --- Transcript Storage ---
    54→
    55→	// ReadTranscript reads the raw transcript bytes for a session.
    56→	ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error)
    57→
    58→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
    59→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
    60→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
    61→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
    62→
    63→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
    64→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
    65→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
    66→
    67→	// --- Legacy methods (will move to optional interfaces in Phase 4) ---
    68→
    69→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none).
    70→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    71→
    72→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks.
    73→	SupportsHooks() bool
    74→
    75→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin.
    76→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    77→
    78→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input.
    79→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    80→
    81→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    82→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    83→
    84→	// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to the session transcript file.
    85→	ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string
    86→
    87→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    88→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    89→
    90→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    91→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    92→
    93→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session.
    94→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    95→}
    96→
    97→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    98→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    99→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
   100→type HookSupport interface {
   101→	Agent
   102→
   103→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
   104→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
   105→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
   106→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
   107→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
   108→
   109→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
   110→	UninstallHooks() error
   111→
   112→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
   113→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
   114→
   115→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
   116→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
   117→}
   118→
   119→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
   120→// HookNames() is now part of the core Agent interface.
   121→// This interface is kept for backward compatibility during migration.
   122→type HookHandler interface {
   123→	Agent
   124→
   125→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
   126→	GetHookNames() []string
   127→}
   128→
   129→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   130→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   131→// to detect session activity.
   132→type FileWatcher interface {
   133→	Agent
   134→
   135→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   136→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   137→
   138→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   139→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   140→}
   141→
   142→// TranscriptAnalyzer provides format-specific transcript parsing.
   143→// Agents that implement this get richer checkpoints (transcript-derived file lists,
   144→// prompts, summaries). Agents that don't still participate in the checkpoint lifecycle
   145→// via git-status-based file detection and raw transcript storage.
   146→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   147→	Agent
   148→
   149→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   150→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   151→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   152→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   153→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   154→
   155→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   156→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   157→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   158→	// Returns:
   159→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   160→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   161→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   162→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   163→
   164→	// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   165→	ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error)
   166→
   167→	// ExtractSummary extracts a summary of the session from the transcript.
   168→	ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error)
   169→}
   170→
   171→// TranscriptPreparer is called before ReadTranscript to handle agent-specific
   172→// flush/sync requirements (e.g., Claude Code's async transcript writing).
   173→// The framework calls PrepareTranscript before ReadTranscript if implemented.
   174→type TranscriptPreparer interface {
   175→	Agent
   176→
   177→	// PrepareTranscript ensures the transcript is ready to read.
   178→	// For Claude Code, this waits for the async transcript flush to complete.
   179→	PrepareTranscript(sessionRef string) error
   180→}
   181→
   182→// TokenCalculator provides token usage calculation for a session.
   183→// The framework calls this during step save and checkpoint if implemented.
   184→type TokenCalculator interface {
   185→	Agent
   186→
   187→	// CalculateTokenUsage computes token usage from the transcript starting at the given offset.
   188→	CalculateTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) (*TokenUsage, error)
   189→}
   190→
   191→// SubagentAwareExtractor provides methods for extracting files and tokens including subagents.
   192→// Agents that support spawning subagents (like Claude Code's Task tool) should implement this
   193→// to ensure subagent contributions are included in checkpoints.
   194→type SubagentAwareExtractor interface {
   195→	Agent
   196→
   197→	// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and any spawned subagents.
   198→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   199→	// Returns a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   200→	ExtractAllModifiedFiles(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error)
   201→
   202→	// CalculateTotalTokenUsage computes token usage including all spawned subagents.
   203→	// The subagentsDir parameter specifies where subagent transcripts are stored.
   204→	CalculateTotalTokenUsage(sessionRef string, fromOffset int, subagentsDir string) (*TokenUsage, error)
   205→}

  "content": "package cursor\n\nimport \"encoding/json\"\n\n// CursorHooksFile represents the .cursor/hooks.json structure.\n// Cursor uses a flat JSON file with version and hooks sections.\ntype CursorHooksFile struct {\n\tVersion int         `json:\"version\"`\n\tHooks   CursorHooks `json:\"hooks\"`\n}\n\n// CursorHooks contains all hook configurations using camelCase keys.\ntype CursorHooks struct {\n\tSessionStart      []CursorHookEntry `json:\"sessionStart,omitempty\"`\n\tSessionEnd        []CursorHookEntry `json:\"sessionEnd,omitempty\"`\n\tBeforeSubmitPrompt []CursorHookEntry `json:\"beforeSubmitPrompt,omitempty\"`\n\tStop              []CursorHookEntry `json:\"stop,omitempty\"`\n\tPreToolUse        []CursorHookEntry `json:\"preToolUse,omitempty\"`\n\tPostToolUse       []CursorHookEntry `json:\"postToolUse,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// CursorHookEntry represents a single hook command.\n// Cursor hooks have a command string and an optional matcher field for filtering by tool name.\ntype CursorHookEntry struct {\n\tCommand string `json:\"command\"`\n\tMatcher string `json:\"matcher,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// sessionInfoRaw is the JSON structure from SessionStart/SessionEnd/Stop hooks.\n// Cursor may provide session_id or conversation_id (fallback).\ntype sessionInfoRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tConversationID string `json:\"conversation_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n}\n\n// getSessionID returns session_id if present, falling back to conversation_id.\nfunc (s *sessionInfoRaw) getSessionID() string {\n\tif s.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\treturn s.SessionID\n\t}\n\treturn s.ConversationID\n}\n\n// userPromptSubmitRaw is the JSON structure from BeforeSubmitPrompt hooks.\ntype userPromptSubmitRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tConversationID string `json:\"conversation_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tPrompt         string `json:\"prompt\"`\n}\n\n// getSessionID returns session_id if present, falling back to conversation_id.\nfunc (u *userPromptSubmitRaw) getSessionID() string {\n\tif u.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\treturn u.SessionID\n\t}\n\treturn u.ConversationID\n}\n\n// taskHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PreToolUse[Task] hook.\ntype taskHookInputRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string          `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tConversationID string          `json:\"conversation_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string          `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tToolUseID      string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input\"`\n}\n\n// getSessionID returns session_id if present, falling back to conversation_id.\nfunc (t *taskHookInputRaw) getSessionID() string {\n\tif t.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\treturn t.SessionID\n\t}\n\treturn t.ConversationID\n}\n\n// postToolHookInputRaw is the JSON structure from PostToolUse hooks.\ntype postToolHookInputRaw struct {\n\tSessionID      string          `json:\"session_id\"`\n\tConversationID string          `json:\"conversation_id\"`\n\tTranscriptPath string          `json:\"transcript_path\"`\n\tToolUseID      string          `json:\"tool_use_id\"`\n\tToolInput      json.RawMessage `json:\"tool_input\"`\n\tToolResponse   struct {\n\t\tAgentID string `json:\"agentId\"`\n\t} `json:\"tool_response\"`\n}\n\n// getSessionID returns session_id if present, falling back to conversation_id.\nfunc (p *postToolHookInputRaw) getSessionID() string {\n\tif p.SessionID != \"\" {\n\t\treturn p.SessionID\n\t}\n\treturn p.ConversationID\n}\n\n// Tool names used in Cursor transcripts (same as Claude Code)\nconst (\n\tToolWrite        = \"Write\"\n\tToolEdit         = \"Edit\"\n\tToolNotebookEdit = \"NotebookEdit\"\n)\n\n// FileModificationTools lists tools that create or modify files\nvar FileModificationTools = []string{\n\tToolWrite,\n\tToolEdit,\n\tToolNotebookEdit,\n}\n"
}

{
