  "content": "// Package cursor implements the Agent interface for Cursor.\npackage cursor\n\nimport (\n\t\"bufio\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"regexp\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript\"\n)\n\n//nolint:gochecknoinits // Agent self-registration is the intended pattern\nfunc init() {\n\tagent.Register(agent.AgentNameCursor, NewCursorAgent)\n}\n\n// CursorAgent implements the Agent interface for Cursor.\ntype CursorAgent struct{}\n\n// NewCursorAgent creates a new Cursor agent instance.\nfunc NewCursorAgent() agent.Agent {\n\treturn &CursorAgent{}\n}\n\n// Name returns the agent registry key.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) Name() agent.AgentName {\n\treturn agent.AgentNameCursor\n}\n\n// Type returns the agent type identifier.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) Type() agent.AgentType {\n\treturn agent.AgentTypeCursor\n}\n\n// Description returns a human-readable description.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) Description() string {\n\treturn \"Cursor - AI-powered code editor\"\n}\n\n// DetectPresence checks if Cursor is configured in the repository.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\n\tcursorDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".cursor\")\n\tif _, err := os.Stat(cursorDir); err == nil {\n\t\treturn true, nil\n\t}\n\treturn false, nil\n}\n\n// GetHookConfigPath returns the path to Cursor's hook config file.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetHookConfigPath() string {\n\treturn \".cursor/hooks.json\"\n}\n\n// SupportsHooks returns true as Cursor supports lifecycle hooks.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) SupportsHooks() bool {\n\treturn true\n}\n\n// ParseHookInput parses Cursor hook input from stdin.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"empty input\")\n\t}\n\n\tinput := &agent.HookInput{\n\t\tHookType:  hookType,\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now(),\n\t\tRawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),\n\t}\n\n\tswitch hookType {\n\tcase agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:\n\t\tvar raw userPromptSubmitRaw\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse user prompt submit: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tinput.SessionID = raw.getSessionID()\n\t\tinput.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath\n\t\tinput.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt\n\n\tcase agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:\n\t\tvar raw sessionInfoRaw\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse session info: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tinput.SessionID = raw.getSessionID()\n\t\tinput.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath\n\n\tcase agent.HookPreToolUse:\n\t\tvar raw taskHookInputRaw\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse pre-tool input: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tinput.SessionID = raw.getSessionID()\n\t\tinput.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath\n\t\tinput.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID\n\t\tinput.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput\n\n\tcase agent.HookPostToolUse:\n\t\tvar raw postToolHookInputRaw\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse post-tool input: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tinput.SessionID = raw.getSessionID()\n\t\tinput.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath\n\t\tinput.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID\n\t\tinput.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput\n\t\tif raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\t\tinput.RawData[\"agent_id\"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn input, nil\n}\n\n// GetSessionID extracts the session ID from hook input.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetSessionID(input *agent.HookInput) string {\n\treturn input.SessionID\n}\n\n// ResolveSessionFile returns the path to a Cursor session file.\n// Cursor uses JSONL format like Claude Code.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ResolveSessionFile(sessionDir, agentSessionID string) string {\n\treturn filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+\".jsonl\")\n}\n\n// ProtectedDirs returns directories that Cursor uses for config/state.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ProtectedDirs() []string { return []string{\".cursor\"} }\n\n// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Cursor stores session transcripts.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {\n\tif override := os.Getenv(\"ENTIRE_TEST_CURSOR_PROJECT_DIR\"); override != \"\" {\n\t\treturn override, nil\n\t}\n\n\thomeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get home directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tprojectDir := SanitizePathForCursor(repoPath)\n\treturn filepath.Join(homeDir, \".cursor\", \"projects\", projectDir), nil\n}\n\n// ReadSession reads a session from Cursor's storage (JSONL transcript file).\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {\n\tif input.SessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"session reference (transcript path) is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn &agent.AgentSession{\n\t\tSessionID:     input.SessionID,\n\t\tAgentName:     c.Name(),\n\t\tSessionRef:    input.SessionRef,\n\t\tStartTime:     time.Now(),\n\t\tNativeData:    data,\n\t\tModifiedFiles: extractModifiedFiles(lines),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// WriteSession writes a session to Cursor's storage (JSONL transcript file).\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {\n\tif session == nil {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session is nil\")\n\t}\n\n\tif session.AgentName != \"\" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"session belongs to agent %q, not %q\", session.AgentName, c.Name())\n\t}\n\n\tif session.SessionRef == \"\" {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session reference (transcript path) is required\")\n\t}\n\n\tif len(session.NativeData) == 0 {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"session has no native data to write\")\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Cursor session.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {\n\treturn \"cursor --resume \" + sessionID\n}\n\n// SanitizePathForCursor converts a path to Cursor's project directory format.\nvar nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)\n\nfunc SanitizePathForCursor(path string) string {\n\treturn nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, \"-\")\n}\n\n// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation\n\n// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Cursor transcript.\n// Cursor uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tfile, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Cursor transcript location\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tif os.IsNotExist(err) {\n\t\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open transcript file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\treader := bufio.NewReader(file)\n\tlineCount := 0\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tline, err := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\tif len(line) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\t\tlineCount++\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tlineCount++\n\t}\n\n\treturn lineCount, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {\n\tif path == \"\" {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\tfile, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Cursor transcript location\n\tif openErr != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open transcript file: %w\", openErr)\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\treader := bufio.NewReader(file)\n\tvar lines []transcript.Line\n\tlineNum := 0\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tlineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", readErr)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif len(lineData) > 0 {\n\t\t\tlineNum++\n\t\t\tif lineNum > startOffset {\n\t\t\t\tvar line transcript.Line\n\t\t\t\tif parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tlines = append(lines, line)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif readErr == io.EOF {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn extractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil\n}\n\n// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {\n\tchunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn chunks, nil\n}\n\n// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {\n\treturn agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil\n}\n\n// extractModifiedFiles extracts file paths from transcript lines that contain file-modifying tools.\nfunc extractModifiedFiles(lines []transcript.Line) []string {\n\tseen := make(map[string]bool)\n\tvar files []string\n\n\tfor i := range lines {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar msg transcript.AssistantMessage\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type != transcript.ContentTypeToolUse {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tisModifyTool := false\n\t\t\tfor _, name := range FileModificationTools {\n\t\t\t\tif block.Name == name {\n\t\t\t\t\tisModifyTool = true\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !isModifyTool {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tvar toolInput transcript.ToolInput\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &toolInput); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tfile := toolInput.FilePath\n\t\t\tif file == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tfile = toolInput.NotebookPath\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif file != \"\" && !seen[file] {\n\t\t\t\tseen[file] = true\n\t\t\t\tfiles = append(files, file)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn files\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/lifecycle.go",
  "content": "package cursor\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"errors\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript\"\n)\n\n// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.\nvar _ agent.TranscriptAnalyzer = (*CursorAgent)(nil)\n\n// HookNames returns the hook verbs Cursor supports.\n// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) HookNames() []string {\n\treturn c.GetHookNames()\n}\n\n// ParseHookEvent translates a Cursor hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.\n// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\tswitch hookName {\n\tcase HookNameSessionStart:\n\t\treturn c.parseSessionStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameBeforeSubmitPrompt:\n\t\treturn c.parseTurnStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameStop:\n\t\treturn c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNameSessionEnd:\n\t\treturn c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePreTask:\n\t\treturn c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePostTask:\n\t\treturn c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)\n\tcase HookNamePostTodo:\n\t\t// PostTodo is handled outside the generic dispatcher (incremental checkpoints).\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action\n\t}\n}\n\n// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn data, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {\n\tlines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tvar prompts []string\n\tfor i := range lines {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tcontent := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)\n\t\tif content != \"\" {\n\t\t\tprompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn prompts, nil\n}\n\n// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)\n\tif parseErr != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", parseErr)\n\t}\n\n\tfor i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {\n\t\tif lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg transcript.AssistantMessage\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfor _, block := range msg.Content {\n\t\t\tif block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\treturn block.Text, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn \"\", nil\n}\n\n// --- Internal hook parsing functions ---\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tPrompt:     raw.Prompt,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.TurnEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SessionEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\treturn &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentStart,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}, nil\n}\n\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {\n\traw, err := readAndParse[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err\n\t}\n\tevent := &agent.Event{\n\t\tType:       agent.SubagentEnd,\n\t\tSessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),\n\t\tSessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,\n\t\tToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,\n\t\tToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,\n\t\tTimestamp:  time.Now(),\n\t}\n\tif raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != \"\" {\n\t\tevent.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID\n\t}\n\treturn event, nil\n}\n\n// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.\nfunc readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read hook input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif len(data) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil, errors.New(\"empty hook input\")\n\t}\n\tvar result T\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hook input: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn &result, nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/hooks.go",
  "content": "package cursor\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil\"\n\t\"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths\"\n)\n\n// Ensure CursorAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler\nvar (\n\t_ agent.HookSupport = (*CursorAgent)(nil)\n\t_ agent.HookHandler = (*CursorAgent)(nil)\n)\n\n// Cursor hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks cursor`\nconst (\n\tHookNameSessionStart      = \"session-start\"\n\tHookNameSessionEnd        = \"session-end\"\n\tHookNameBeforeSubmitPrompt = \"before-submit-prompt\"\n\tHookNameStop              = \"stop\"\n\tHookNamePreTask           = \"pre-task\"\n\tHookNamePostTask          = \"post-task\"\n\tHookNamePostTodo          = \"post-todo\"\n)\n\n// CursorHooksFileName is the hooks file used by Cursor.\nconst CursorHooksFileName = \"hooks.json\"\n\n// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks\nvar entireHookPrefixes = []string{\n\t\"entire \",\n\t\"go run ${CURSOR_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go \",\n}\n\n// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs Cursor supports.\n// These become subcommands: entire hooks cursor <verb>\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetHookNames() []string {\n\treturn []string{\n\t\tHookNameSessionStart,\n\t\tHookNameSessionEnd,\n\t\tHookNameBeforeSubmitPrompt,\n\t\tHookNameStop,\n\t\tHookNamePreTask,\n\t\tHookNamePostTask,\n\t\tHookNamePostTodo,\n\t}\n}\n\n// InstallHooks installs Cursor hooks in .cursor/hooks.json.\n// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.\n// Returns the number of hooks installed.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get current directory: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".cursor\", CursorHooksFileName)\n\n\t// Read existing hooks file if it exists\n\tvar hooksFile CursorHooksFile\n\n\texistingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(hooksPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path\n\tif readErr == nil {\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &hooksFile); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse existing hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\thooksFile.Version = 1\n\t}\n\n\t// If force is true, remove all existing Entire hooks first\n\tif force {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart)\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd)\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt)\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop)\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse)\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse)\n\t}\n\n\t// Define hook commands\n\tvar cmdPrefix string\n\tif localDev {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"go run ${CURSOR_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks cursor \"\n\t} else {\n\t\tcmdPrefix = \"entire hooks cursor \"\n\t}\n\n\tsessionStartCmd := cmdPrefix + \"session-start\"\n\tsessionEndCmd := cmdPrefix + \"session-end\"\n\tbeforeSubmitPromptCmd := cmdPrefix + \"before-submit-prompt\"\n\tstopCmd := cmdPrefix + \"stop\"\n\tpreTaskCmd := cmdPrefix + \"pre-task\"\n\tpostTaskCmd := cmdPrefix + \"post-task\"\n\tpostTodoCmd := cmdPrefix + \"post-todo\"\n\n\tcount := 0\n\n\t// Add hooks if they don't exist\n\tif !hookCommandExists(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart, sessionStartCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart = append(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart, CursorHookEntry{Command: sessionStartCmd})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd, sessionEndCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd = append(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd, CursorHookEntry{Command: sessionEndCmd})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt, beforeSubmitPromptCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt = append(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt, CursorHookEntry{Command: beforeSubmitPromptCmd})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop, stopCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.Stop = append(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop, CursorHookEntry{Command: stopCmd})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse, \"Task\", preTaskCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse = append(hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse, CursorHookEntry{Command: preTaskCmd, Matcher: \"Task\"})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, \"Task\", postTaskCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse = append(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, CursorHookEntry{Command: postTaskCmd, Matcher: \"Task\"})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, \"TodoWrite\", postTodoCmd) {\n\t\thooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse = append(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, CursorHookEntry{Command: postTodoCmd, Matcher: \"TodoWrite\"})\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\n\tif count == 0 {\n\t\treturn 0, nil\n\t}\n\n\t// Write to file\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(hooksPath), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create .cursor directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\toutput, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(hooksFile, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn count, nil\n}\n\n// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Cursor hooks.json.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) UninstallHooks() error {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".cursor\", CursorHooksFileName)\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil //nolint:nilerr // No hooks file means nothing to uninstall\n\t}\n\n\tvar hooksFile CursorHooksFile\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hooksFile); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types\n\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart)\n\thooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd)\n\thooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt)\n\thooksFile.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop)\n\thooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse)\n\thooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse)\n\n\t// Write back\n\toutput, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(hooksFile, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write hooks.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\trepoRoot = \".\"\n\t}\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".cursor\", CursorHooksFileName)\n\tdata, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\tvar hooksFile CursorHooksFile\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hooksFile); err != nil {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\n\treturn hasEntireHook(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop) ||\n\t\thasEntireHook(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart) ||\n\t\thasEntireHook(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt)\n}\n\n// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Cursor supports.\nfunc (c *CursorAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {\n\treturn []agent.HookType{\n\t\tagent.HookSessionStart,\n\t\tagent.HookSessionEnd,\n\t\tagent.HookUserPromptSubmit,\n\t\tagent.HookStop,\n\t\tagent.HookPreToolUse,\n\t\tagent.HookPostToolUse,\n\t}\n}\n\n// Helper functions for hook management\n\nfunc hookCommandExists(entries []CursorHookEntry, command string) bool {\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Command == command {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(entries []CursorHookEntry, matcher, command string) bool {\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Matcher == matcher && entry.Command == command {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc isEntireHook(command string) bool {\n\tfor _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {\n\t\tif strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc hasEntireHook(entries []CursorHookEntry) bool {\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif isEntireHook(entry.Command) {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}\n\nfunc removeEntireHooks(entries []CursorHookEntry) []CursorHookEntry {\n\tresult := make([]CursorHookEntry, 0, len(entries))\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif !isEntireHook(entry.Command) {\n\t\t\tresult = append(result, entry)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn result\n}\n"
}

    13→// metadataDenyRuleTest is the rule that blocks Claude from reading Entire metadata
    14→const metadataDenyRuleTest = "Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"

    16→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_FreshInstall(t *testing.T) {
    17→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    18→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    19→
    20→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    21→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    22→	if err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    27→
    28→	// Verify permissions.deny contains our rule
    29→	if !containsRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
    30→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain %q", perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest)
    31→	}
    32→}
    33→
    34→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_Idempotent(t *testing.T) {
    35→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
    36→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
    37→
    38→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
    39→	// First install
    40→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    41→	if err != nil {
    42→		t.Fatalf("first InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Second install
    46→	_, err = agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		t.Fatalf("second InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
    49→	}
    50→
    51→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
    52→
    53→	// Count occurrences of our rule
    54→	count := 0
    55→	for _, rule := range perms.Deny {
    56→		if rule == metadataDenyRuleTest {
    57→			count++
    58→		}
    59→	}
    60→	if count != 1 {
    61→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny contains %d copies of rule, want 1", count)
    62→	}
    63→}

   149→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUnknownFields(t *testing.T) {
   150→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   151→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   152→
   153→	// Create settings.json with unknown permission fields like "ask"
   154→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   155→  "permissions": {
   156→    "allow": ["Read(**)"],
   157→    "ask": ["Write(**)", "Bash(*)"],
   158→    "customField": {"nested": "value"}
   159→  }
   160→}`)
   161→
   162→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   163→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Read raw settings to check for unknown fields
   169→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   170→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   171→	if err != nil {
   172→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   173→	}
   174→
   175→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   176→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   177→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   181→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawSettings["permissions"], &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   182→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions: %v", err)
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Verify "ask" field is preserved
   186→	if _, ok := rawPermissions["ask"]; !ok {
   187→		t.Errorf("permissions.ask was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawPermissions))
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Verify "customField" is preserved
   191→	if _, ok := rawPermissions["customField"]; !ok {
   192→		t.Errorf("permissions.customField was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawPermissions))
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	// Verify the "ask" field content
   196→	var askRules []string
   197→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["ask"], &askRules); err != nil {
   198→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.ask: %v", err)
   199→	}
   200→	if len(askRules) != 2 || askRules[0] != "Write(**)" || askRules[1] != "Bash(*)" {
   201→		t.Errorf("permissions.ask = %v, want [Write(**), Bash(*)]", askRules)
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Verify the deny rule was added
   205→	var denyRules []string
   206→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["deny"], &denyRules); err != nil {
   207→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.deny: %v", err)
   208→	}
   209→	if !containsRule(denyRules, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
   210→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain %q", denyRules, metadataDenyRuleTest)
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Verify "allow" is preserved
   214→	var allowRules []string
   215→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["allow"], &allowRules); err != nil {
   216→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.allow: %v", err)
   217→	}
   218→	if len(allowRules) != 1 || allowRules[0] != "Read(**)" {
   219→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want [Read(**)]", allowRules)
   220→	}
   221→}

   223→// Helper functions
   224→
   225→// testPermissions is used only for test assertions
   226→type testPermissions struct {
   227→	Allow []string `json:"allow,omitempty"`
   228→	Deny  []string `json:"deny,omitempty"`
   229→}
   230→
   231→func readPermissions(t *testing.T, tempDir string) testPermissions {
   232→	t.Helper()
   233→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   234→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   237→	}
   238→
   239→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   240→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   241→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	var perms testPermissions
   245→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   246→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &perms); err != nil {
   247→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions: %v", err)
   248→		}
   249→	}
   250→	return perms
   251→}
   252→
   253→func writeSettingsFile(t *testing.T, tempDir, content string) {
   254→	t.Helper()
   255→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude")
   256→	if err := os.MkdirAll(claudeDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   257→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .claude dir: %v", err)
   258→	}
   259→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(claudeDir, "settings.json")
   260→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   261→		t.Fatalf("failed to write settings.json: %v", err)
   262→	}
   263→}
   264→
   265→func containsRule(rules []string, rule string) bool {
   266→	return slices.Contains(rules, rule)
   267→}
   268→
   269→func TestUninstallHooks(t *testing.T) {
   270→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   271→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   272→
   273→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   274→
   275→	// First install
   276→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   277→	if err != nil {
   278→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Verify hooks are installed
   282→	if !agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   283→		t.Error("hooks should be installed before uninstall")
   284→	}
   285→
   286→	// Uninstall
   287→	err = agent.UninstallHooks()
   288→	if err != nil {
   289→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Verify hooks are removed
   293→	if agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   294→		t.Error("hooks should not be installed after uninstall")
   295→	}
   296→}
   297→

   311→func TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserHooks(t *testing.T) {
   312→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   313→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   314→
   315→	// Create settings with both user and entire hooks
   316→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   317→  "hooks": {
   318→    "Stop": [
   319→      {
   320→        "matcher": "",
   321→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user hook"}]
   322→      },
   323→      {
   324→        "matcher": "",
   325→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code stop"}]
   326→      }
   327→    ]
   328→  }
   329→}`)
   330→
   331→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   332→	err := agent.UninstallHooks()
   333→	if err != nil {
   334→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   335→	}
   336→
   337→	settings := readClaudeSettings(t, tempDir)
   338→
   339→	// Verify only user hooks remain
   340→	if len(settings.Hooks.Stop) != 1 {
   341→		t.Errorf("Stop hooks = %d after uninstall, want 1 (user only)", len(settings.Hooks.Stop))
   342→	}
   343→
   344→	// Verify it's the user hook
   345→	if len(settings.Hooks.Stop) > 0 && len(settings.Hooks.Stop[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   346→		if settings.Hooks.Stop[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo user hook" {
   347→			t.Error("user hook was removed during uninstall")
   348→		}
   349→	}
   350→}

   420→// readClaudeSettings reads and parses the Claude Code settings file
   421→func readClaudeSettings(t *testing.T, tempDir string) ClaudeSettings {
   422→	t.Helper()
   423→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   424→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   425→	if err != nil {
   426→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   427→	}
   428→
   429→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   430→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   431→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   432→	}
   433→	return settings
   434→}

   436→//nolint:tparallel // Parent uses t.Chdir() which prevents t.Parallel(); subtests only read from pre-loaded data
   437→func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType(t *testing.T) {
   438→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   439→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   440→
   441→	// Create settings with user hooks on the same hook types we use
   442→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   443→  "hooks": {
   444→    "Stop": [
   445→      {
   446→        "matcher": "",
   447→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user stop hook"}]
   448→      }
   449→    ],
   450→    "SessionStart": [
   451→      {
   452→        "matcher": "",
   453→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user session start"}]
   454→      }
   455→    ],
   456→    "PostToolUse": [
   457→      {
   458→        "matcher": "Write",
   459→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user wrote file"}]
   460→      }
   461→    ]
   462→  }
   463→}`)
   464→
   465→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   466→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   467→	if err != nil {
   468→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   469→	}
   470→
   471→	rawHooks := testutil.ReadRawHooks(t, tempDir, ".claude")
   472→
   473→	t.Run("Stop", func(t *testing.T) {
   474→		t.Parallel()
   475→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   476→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["Stop"], &matchers); err != nil {
   477→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse Stop hooks: %v", err)
   478→		}
   479→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "echo user stop hook", "user Stop hook")
   480→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "entire hooks claude-code stop", "Entire Stop hook")
   481→	})
   482→
   483→	t.Run("SessionStart", func(t *testing.T) {
   484→		t.Parallel()
   485→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   486→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["SessionStart"], &matchers); err != nil {
   487→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse SessionStart hooks: %v", err)
   488→		}
   489→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "echo user session start", "user SessionStart hook")
   490→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "", "entire hooks claude-code session-start", "Entire SessionStart hook")
   491→	})
   492→
   493→	t.Run("PostToolUse", func(t *testing.T) {
   494→		t.Parallel()
   495→		var matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   496→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["PostToolUse"], &matchers); err != nil {
   497→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse PostToolUse hooks: %v", err)
   498→		}
   499→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "Write", "echo user wrote file", "user Write hook")
   500→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "Task", "entire hooks claude-code post-task", "Entire Task hook")
   501→		assertHookExists(t, matchers, "TodoWrite", "entire hooks claude-code post-todo", "Entire TodoWrite hook")
   502→	})
   503→}
   504→
   505→// assertHookExists checks that a hook with the given matcher and command exists
   506→func assertHookExists(t *testing.T, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcher, command, description string) {
   507→	t.Helper()
   508→	for _, m := range matchers {
   509→		if m.Matcher == matcher {
   510→			for _, h := range m.Hooks {
   511→				if h.Command == command {
   512→					return
   513→				}
   514→			}
   515→		}
   516→	}
   517→	t.Errorf("%s was not found (matcher=%q, command=%q)", description, matcher, command)
   518→}
   519→
   520→func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes(t *testing.T) {
   521→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   522→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   523→
   524→	// Create settings with a hook type we don't handle (Notification is a real Claude Code hook type)
   525→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   526→  "hooks": {
   527→    "Notification": [
   528→      {
   529→        "matcher": "",
   530→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo notification received"}]
   531→      }
   532→    ],
   533→    "SubagentStop": [
   534→      {
   535→        "matcher": ".*",
   536→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo subagent stopped"}]
   537→      }
   538→    ]
   539→  }
   540→}`)
   541→
   542→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   543→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   544→	if err != nil {
   545→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	// Read raw settings to check for unknown hook types
   549→	rawHooks := testutil.ReadRawHooks(t, tempDir, ".claude")
   550→
   551→	// Verify Notification hook is preserved
   552→	if _, ok := rawHooks["Notification"]; !ok {
   553→		t.Errorf("Notification hook type was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawHooks))
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Verify SubagentStop hook is preserved
   557→	if _, ok := rawHooks["SubagentStop"]; !ok {
   558→		t.Errorf("SubagentStop hook type was not preserved, got keys: %v", testutil.GetKeys(rawHooks))
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	// Verify the Notification hook content is intact
   562→	var notificationMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   563→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["Notification"], &notificationMatchers); err != nil {
   564→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse Notification hooks: %v", err)
   565→	}
   566→	if len(notificationMatchers) != 1 {
   567→		t.Errorf("Notification matchers = %d, want 1", len(notificationMatchers))
   568→	}
   569→	if len(notificationMatchers) > 0 && len(notificationMatchers[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   570→		if notificationMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo notification received" {
   571→			t.Errorf("Notification hook command = %q, want %q",
   572→				notificationMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command, "echo notification received")
   573→		}
   574→	}
   575→
   576→	// Verify the SubagentStop hook content is intact
   577→	var subagentStopMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher
   578→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["SubagentStop"], &subagentStopMatchers); err != nil {
   579→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse SubagentStop hooks: %v", err)
   580→	}
   581→	if len(subagentStopMatchers) != 1 {
   582→		t.Errorf("SubagentStop matchers = %d, want 1", len(subagentStopMatchers))
   583→	}
   584→	if len(subagentStopMatchers) > 0 {
   585→		if subagentStopMatchers[0].Matcher != ".*" {
   586→			t.Errorf("SubagentStop matcher = %q, want %q", subagentStopMatchers[0].Matcher, ".*")
   587→		}
   588→		if len(subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   589→			if subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo subagent stopped" {
   590→				t.Errorf("SubagentStop hook command = %q, want %q",
   591→					subagentStopMatchers[0].Hooks[0].Command, "echo subagent stopped")
   592→			}
   593→		}
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Verify our hooks were also installed
   597→	if _, ok := rawHooks["Stop"]; !ok {
   598→		t.Errorf("Stop hook should have been installed")
   599→	}
   600→}
   601→
   602→func TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes(t *testing.T) {
   603→	tempDir := t.TempDir()

   605→

   653→		if err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks["Stop"], &stopMatchers); err == nil && len(stopMatchers) > 0 {

   275→func TestParseHookEvent_AllHookTypes(t *testing.T) {
   276→	t.Parallel()
   277→
   278→	testCases := []struct {
   279→		hookName      string
   280→		expectedType  agent.EventType
   281→		expectNil     bool
   282→		inputTemplate string
   283→	}{
   284→		{
   285→			hookName:      HookNameSessionStart,
   286→			expectedType:  agent.SessionStart,
   287→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s1", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   288→		},
   289→		{
   290→			hookName:      HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
   291→			expectedType:  agent.TurnStart,
   292→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s2", "transcript_path": "/t", "prompt": "hi"}`,
   293→		},
   294→		{
   295→			hookName:      HookNameStop,
   296→			expectedType:  agent.TurnEnd,
   297→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s3", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   298→		},
   299→		{
   300→			hookName:      HookNameSessionEnd,
   301→			expectedType:  agent.SessionEnd,
   302→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s4", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   303→		},
   304→		{
   305→			hookName:      HookNamePreTask,
   306→			expectedType:  agent.SubagentStart,
   307→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s5", "transcript_path": "/t", "tool_use_id": "t1", "tool_input": {}}`,
   308→		},
   309→		{
   310→			hookName:      HookNamePostTask,
   311→			expectedType:  agent.SubagentEnd,
   312→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s6", "transcript_path": "/t", "tool_use_id": "t2", "tool_input": {}, "tool_response": {}}`,
   313→		},
   314→		{
   315→			hookName:      HookNamePostTodo,
   316→			expectNil:     true,
   317→			inputTemplate: `{"session_id": "s7", "transcript_path": "/t"}`,
   318→		},
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	for _, tc := range testCases {
   322→		t.Run(tc.hookName, func(t *testing.T) {
   323→			t.Parallel()
   324→
   325→			ag := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   326→			event, err := ag.ParseHookEvent(tc.hookName, strings.NewReader(tc.inputTemplate))
   327→
   328→			if err != nil {
   329→				t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   330→			}
   331→
   332→			if tc.expectNil {
   333→				if event != nil {
   334→					t.Errorf("expected nil event, got %+v", event)
   335→				}
   336→				return
   337→			}
   338→
   339→			if event == nil {
   340→				t.Fatal("expected event, got nil")
   341→			}
   342→			if event.Type != tc.expectedType {
   343→				t.Errorf("expected event type %v, got %v", tc.expectedType, event.Type)
   344→			}
   345→		})
   346→	}
   347→}

   352→	input := `{"session_id": "test-123", "transcript_path": "/path/to/transcript"}`

   377→	}
   378→	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "empty hook input") {
   379→		t.Errorf("expected 'empty hook input' error, got: %v", err)
   380→	}
   381→}
   382→
   383→func TestReadAndParse_InvalidJSON(t *testing.T) {
   384→	t.Parallel()
   385→
   386→	_, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](strings.NewReader("not valid json"))
   387→
   388→	if err == nil {
   389→		t.Fatal("expected error for invalid JSON")
   390→	}
   391→	if !strings.Contains(err.Error(), "failed to parse hook input") {
   392→		t.Errorf("expected 'failed to parse hook input' error, got: %v", err)
   393→	}
   394→}
   395→
   396→func TestReadAndParse_PartialJSON(t *testing.T) {
   397→	t.Parallel()
   398→
   399→	// JSON with only some fields - should still parse (missing fields are zero values)
   400→	input := `{"session_id": "partial-only"}`
   401→
   402→	result, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](strings.NewReader(input))
   403→
   404→	if err != nil {
   405→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   406→	}
   407→	if result.SessionID != "partial-only" {
   408→		t.Errorf("expected session_id 'partial-only', got %q", result.SessionID)
   409→	}
   410→	if result.TranscriptPath != "" {
   411→		t.Errorf("expected empty transcript_path, got %q", result.TranscriptPath)
   412→	}
   413→}
   414→
   415→func TestReadAndParse_ExtraFields(t *testing.T) {
   416→	t.Parallel()
   417→
   418→	// JSON with extra fields - should ignore them
   419→	input := `{"session_id": "test", "transcript_path": "/t", "extra_field": "ignored", "another": 123}`
   420→
   421→	result, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](strings.NewReader(input))
   422→
   423→	if err != nil {
   424→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   425→	}
   426→	if result.SessionID != "test" {
   427→		t.Errorf("expected session_id 'test', got %q", result.SessionID)
   428→	}
   429→}

     1→// Package testutil provides shared test utilities for agent packages.
     2→package testutil
     3→
     4→import (
     5→	"encoding/json"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"testing"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// ReadRawHooks reads the raw hooks map from a settings file.
    12→// settingsDir is the directory name (e.g., ".claude" or ".gemini").
    13→func ReadRawHooks(t *testing.T, tempDir, settingsDir string) map[string]json.RawMessage {
    14→	t.Helper()
    15→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, settingsDir, "settings.json")
    16→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // Test utility, path constructed from test tempDir
    17→	if err != nil {
    18→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
    19→	}
    20→
    21→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    22→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    23→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
    24→	}
    25→
    26→	var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
    27→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    28→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {
    29→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse hooks: %v", err)
    30→		}
    31→	}
    32→	return rawHooks
    33→}
    34→
    35→// GetKeys returns the keys of a map as a slice.
    36→func GetKeys(m map[string]json.RawMessage) []string {
    37→	keys := make([]string, 0, len(m))
    38→	for k := range m {
    39→		keys = append(keys, k)
    40→	}
    41→	return keys
    42→}
    43→

len(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse))\n\t}\n\tassertEntryWithMatcher(t, hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, \"Write\", \"echo file written\")\n\tassertEntryWithMatcher(t, hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, \"Task\", \"entire hooks cursor post-task\")\n\tassertEntryWithMatcher(t, hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse, \"TodoWrite\", \"entire hooks cursor post-todo\")\n}\n\nfunc TestInstallHooks_LocalDev(t *testing.T) {\n\ttempDir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(tempDir)\n\n\tag := &CursorAgent{}\n\t_, err := ag.InstallHooks(true, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"InstallHooks(localDev=true) error = %v\", hooks.json: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn hooksFile\n}\n\nfunc writeHooksFile(t *testing.T, tempDir string, hooksFile CursorHooksFile) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tcursorDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, \".cursor\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(cursorDir, 0o755); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to create .cursor dir: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\tdata, err := json.MarshalIndent(hooksFile, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to marshal hooks.json: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\thooksPath := filepath.Join(cursorDir, \"hooks.json\")\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, data, 0o644); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to write hooks.json: %v\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc assertEntryCommand(t *testing.T, entries []CursorHookEntry, command string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Command == command {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tt.Errorf(\"hook with command %q not found\", command)\n}\n\nfunc assertEntryWithMatcher(t *testing.T, entries []CursorHookEntry, matcher, command string) {\n\tt.Helper()\n\tfor _, entry := range entries {\n\t\tif entry.Matcher == matcher && entry.Command == command {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tt.Errorf(\"hook with matcher=%q command=%q not found\", matcher, command)\n}\n"

> AGENT

The gocritic lint wants a switch statement. But this is an if-else chain with different conditions, not all on the same variable. However, I notice that the Cursor post-todo handler actually has a problem: `cmd.InOrStdin()` was already read by `ParseHookEvent` above. The Claude handler uses `os.Stdin` directly (which also has the same issue but works because `ParseHookEvent` returned `nil,nil` for post-todo). Let me check how Claude's handler works...

Looking at Claude's `handleClaudeCodePostTodo()` - it reads from `os.Stdin` directly. And `ParseHookEvent` for `post-todo` returns `nil, nil` without reading stdin (it doesn't consume the data). But for Cursor, I was passing `cmd.InOrStdin()` to `handleCursorPostTodo`, which is a problem because `ParseHookEvent` already consumed stdin.

Actually wait - `ParseHookEvent` for `post-todo` returns `nil, nil` immediately without reading stdin. So stdin is still available. But `cmd.InOrStdin()` is a reader that can only be read once. The Claude handler avoids this by reading from `os.Stdin` directly in `handleClaudeCodePostTodo()`.

Let me fix the Cursor handler to also read from `os.Stdin`:

> TOOL

}

     2→// This reuses the same incremental checkpoint logic as Claude Code's PostTodo handler.
     3→package cli
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"io"
     7→)

> AGENT

Actually, wait. `cmd.InOrStdin()` returns the configured stdin for the command, which by default IS `os.Stdin`. Since `ParseHookEvent` returns `nil, nil` without reading for post-todo, the stdin stream is still available. But the safer approach is to match Claude's pattern: `handleClaudeCodePostTodo()` reads from `os.Stdin`. Let me simplify:

}

> AGENT

Here's a summary of everything implemented:

## New Files Created

### `cmd/entire/cli/agent/cursor/` (6 files)

1. **`types.go`** - Cursor-specific types: `CursorHooksFile`, `CursorHooks`, `CursorHookEntry`, stdin payload structs with `conversation_id` fallback support
2. **`cursor.go`** - Main `CursorAgent` implementation with all 18 Agent interface methods + `TranscriptAnalyzer`. Uses JSONL format (same as Claude Code), delegates chunking to `agent.ChunkJSONL`/`ReassembleJSONL`
3. **`lifecycle.go`** - `ParseHookEvent` mapping Cursor's 7 hooks to lifecycle events, `ReadTranscript`, `ExtractPrompts`, `ExtractSummary`, and `readAndParse[T]` generic helper
4. **`hooks.go`** - `HookSupport`/`HookHandler` implementation: `InstallHooks` writes `.cursor/hooks.json`, `UninstallHooks` removes Entire entries, `AreHooksInstalled` checks for our hooks. Supports `matcher` field for pre-task/post-task/post-todo
5. **`hooks_test.go`** - 9 tests: fresh install, idempotent, not installed, after install, uninstall, no hooks file, force reinstall, preserves existing hooks, local dev mode
6. **`lifecycle_test.go`** - 13 tests: all 7 hook types, empty input error, conversation_id fallback (5 subtests), malformed JSON, comprehensive all-hooks-types table test

### `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_cursor_posttodo.go`
Post-todo handler that delegates to the existing Claude Code handler (same incremental checkpoint logic)

## Files Modified

1. **`agent/registry.go`** - Added `AgentNameCursor`/`AgentTypeCursor` constants
2. **`hooks_cmd.go`** - Added `_ "cursor"` import for auto-registration
3. **`hook_registry.go`** - Added Cursor post-todo dispatch, refactored if-else to switch (lint fix), added cursor import
4. **`setup.go`** - Added "(Preview)" label for Cursor, added Cursor to `removeAgentHooks`
5. **`summarize/summarize.go`** - Added `AgentTypeCursor` to exhaustive switch

## Hook Name → Event Mapping

| Cursor Hook | Event | CLI Command |
|---|---|---|
| `session-start` | `SessionStart` | `entire hooks cursor session-start` |
| `before-submit-prompt` | `TurnStart` | `entire hooks cursor before-submit-prompt` |
| `stop` | `TurnEnd` | `entire hooks cursor stop` |
| `session-end` | `SessionEnd` | `entire hooks cursor session-end` |
| `pre-task` | `SubagentStart` | `entire hooks cursor pre-task` |
| `post-task` | `SubagentEnd` | `entire hooks cursor post-task` |
| `post-todo` | nil (handled directly) | `entire hooks cursor post-todo` |

## Verification

- `mise run fmt` - clean
- `mise run lint` - 0 issues
- `mise run test:ci` - all unit + integration tests pass
- `go build ./cmd/entire/` - compiles
- `entire hooks cursor --help` - shows hook subcommands

TurnStart, TurnEnd, SessionEnd, SubagentStart, SubagentEnd)\n- Implements hook installation/uninstallation for `.cursor/hooks.json` with matcher support for tool-specific hooks\n- Reuses JSONL transcript infrastructure (same format as Claude Code) and the existing post-todo incremental checkpoint logic\n\n### Files created (6)\n- `agent/cursor/types.go` — Cursor-specific types with `conversation_id` fallback\n- `agent/cursor/cursor.go` — Main agent implementation (Agent + TranscriptAnalyzer interfaces)\n- `agent/cursor/lifecycle.go` — `ParseHookEvent` with 7 hook mappings\n- `agent/cursor/hooks.go` — Hook installation for `.cursor/hooks.json`\n- `agent/cursor/hooks_test.go` — 9 hook tests\n- `agent/cursor/lifecycle_test.go` — 13 lifecycle tests\n\n### Files modified (6)\n- `agent/registry.go` — Added `AgentNameCursor`/`AgentTypeCursor` constants\n- `hooks_cmd.go` — Blank import to trigger `init()` registration\n- `hook_registry.go` — Cursor post-todo dispatch case\n- `hooks_cursor_posttodo.go` — Post-todo handler (delegates to Claude's)\n- `setup.go` — Preview label + `removeAgentHooks` for Cursor\n- `summarize/summarize.go` — Exhaustive switch case for `AgentTypeCursor`\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] Unit tests pass (`mise run test`)\n- [x] Integration tests pass (`mise run test:integration`)\n- [x] Lint clean (`mise run lint`)\n- [x] Builds successfully (`go build ./cmd/entire/`)\n- [ ] Manual: `entire enable --agent cursor` creates `.cursor/hooks.json`\n- [ ] Manual: `entire disable` removes hooks from `.cursor/hooks.json`\n- [ ] Manual: `entire hooks cursor --help` shows all hook verbs\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",

   160→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Cursor hooks.json.
   161→func (c *CursorAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   162→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   163→	if err != nil {
   164→		repoRoot = "."
   165→	}
   166→	hooksPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".cursor", HooksFileName)
   167→	data, err := os.ReadFile(hooksPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No hooks file means nothing to uninstall
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	var hooksFile CursorHooksFile
   173→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &hooksFile); err != nil {
   174→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks.json: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   178→	hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionStart)
   179→	hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   180→	hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.BeforeSubmitPrompt)
   181→	hooksFile.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.Stop)
   182→	hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PreToolUse)
   183→	hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooks(hooksFile.Hooks.PostToolUse)
   184→
   185→	// Write back
   186→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(hooksFile, "", "  ")
   187→	if err != nil {
   188→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks.json: %w", err)
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	if err := os.WriteFile(hooksPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   192→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write hooks.json: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→	return nil
   195→}
   196→
   197→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.

```
     1→package textutil
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"regexp"
     5→	"strings"
     6→)
     7→
     8→// ideContextTagRegex matches IDE-injected context tags like <ide_opened_file>...</ide_opened_file>
     9→// and <ide_selection>...</ide_selection>. These are injected by the VSCode extension.
    10→var ideContextTagRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<ide_[^>]*>.*?</ide_[^>]*>`)
    11→
    12→// systemTagRegexes matches system-injected context tags that shouldn't appear in user-facing text.
    13→// Each tag type needs its own regex since Go's regexp doesn't support backreferences.
    14→var systemTagRegexes = []*regexp.Regexp{
    15→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<local-command-caveat[^>]*>.*?</local-command-caveat>`),
    16→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<system-reminder[^>]*>.*?</system-reminder>`),
    17→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<command-name[^>]*>.*?</command-name>`),
    18→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<command-message[^>]*>.*?</command-message>`),
    19→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<command-args[^>]*>.*?</command-args>`),
    20→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<local-command-stdout[^>]*>.*?</local-command-stdout>`),
    21→	regexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<user_query[^>]*>.*?</user_query>`),
    22→}
    23→
    24→// StripIDEContextTags removes IDE-injected context tags from prompt text.
    25→// The VSCode extension injects tags like:
    26→//   - <ide_opened_file>...</ide_opened_file> - currently open file
    27→//   - <ide_selection>...</ide_selection> - selected code in editor
    28→//
    29→// Also removes system-injected tags like:
    30→//   - <local-command-caveat>...</local-command-caveat>
    31→//   - <system-reminder>...</system-reminder>
    32→//   - <command-name>...</command-name>
    33→//
    34→// These shouldn't appear in commit messages or session descriptions.
    35→func StripIDEContextTags(text string) string {
    36→	result := ideContextTagRegex.ReplaceAllString(text, "")
    37→	for _, re := range systemTagRegexes {
    38→		result = re.ReplaceAllString(result, "")
    39→	}
    40→	return strings.TrimSpace(result)
    41→}
    42→

> AGENT

The `"cursor user input"` test case expects `<user_query>` to be stripped but its **content** preserved. Currently the `user_query` regex in `systemTagRegexes` strips the tag and its content entirely.

The fix: `<user_query>` should unwrap (keep inner text) rather than remove entirely. It needs to be handled separately from the other system tags which get fully removed.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01KihqkoKro7QEUodjKvaZHk
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/robin/Developer/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil/ide_tags.go",
  "old_string": "\tregexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<user_query[^>]*>.*?</user_query>`),\n}\n\n// StripIDEContextTags removes IDE-injected context tags from prompt text.\n// The VSCode extension injects tags like:\n//   - <ide_opened_file>...</ide_opened_file> - currently open file\n//   - <ide_selection>...</ide_selection> - selected code in editor\n//\n// Also removes system-injected tags like:\n//   - <local-command-caveat>...</local-command-caveat>\n//   - <system-reminder>...</system-reminder>\n//   - <command-name>...</command-name>\n//\n// These shouldn't appear in commit messages or session descriptions.\nfunc StripIDEContextTags(text string) string {\n\tresult := ideContextTagRegex.ReplaceAllString(text, \"\")\n\tfor _, re := range systemTagRegexes {\n\t\tresult = re.ReplaceAllString(result, \"\")\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(result)\n}",
  "new_string": "}\n\n// unwrapTagRegexes matches tags whose content should be kept (unwrapped) rather than removed.\nvar unwrapTagRegexes = []*regexp.Regexp{\n\tregexp.MustCompile(`(?s)<user_query[^>]*>(.*?)</user_query>`),\n}\n\n// StripIDEContextTags removes IDE-injected context tags from prompt text.\n// The VSCode extension injects tags like:\n//   - <ide_opened_file>...</ide_opened_file> - currently open file\n//   - <ide_selection>...</ide_selection> - selected code in editor\n//\n// Also removes system-injected tags like:\n//   - <local-command-caveat>...</local-command-caveat>\n//   - <system-reminder>...</system-reminder>\n//   - <command-name>...</command-name>\n//\n// And unwraps content from wrapper tags like:\n//   - <user_query>...</user_query> - Cursor's user input wrapper\n//\n// These shouldn't appear in commit messages or session descriptions.\nfunc StripIDEContextTags(text string) string {\n\tresult := ideContextTagRegex.ReplaceAllString(text, \"\")\n\tfor _, re := range systemTagRegexes {\n\t\tresult = re.ReplaceAllString(result, \"\")\n\t}\n\tfor _, re := range unwrapTagRegexes {\n\t\tresult = re.ReplaceAllString(result, \"$1\")\n\t}\n\treturn strings.TrimSpace(result)\n}"
}
```

> AGENT

All passing. The issue was that `<user_query>` was being treated like other system tags (fully removed), but it's a wrapper tag — its content is the actual user prompt. Moved it to a separate `unwrapTagRegexes` list that replaces the tag with its captured inner content (`$1`) instead of removing it entirely.

   638→// updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition updates the session state with the new transcript position
   639→// for the auto-commit strategy.
   640→func updateAutoCommitTranscriptPosition(sessionID string, newPosition int) {
   641→	sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)

   643→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)

   648→			SessionID: sessionID,

   650→	}
   651→	sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = newPosition
   652→	sessionState.StepCount++
   653→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   654→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   655→	} else {
   656→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   657→			newPosition, sessionState.StepCount)
   658→	}
   659→}

     1→// lifecycle.go implements the generic lifecycle event dispatcher.
     2→// It routes normalized events from any agent to the appropriate framework actions.
     3→//
     4→// The dispatcher inverts the current flow from "agent handler calls framework functions"
     5→// to "framework dispatcher calls agent methods." Agents are passive data providers;
     6→// the dispatcher handles all orchestration: state transitions, strategy calls,
     7→// file change detection, metadata generation.

    30→// Returns nil if the event was handled successfully.
    31→func DispatchLifecycleEvent(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    32→	if ag == nil {

    41→		return handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag, event)
    42→	case agent.TurnStart:
    43→		return handleLifecycleTurnStart(ag, event)
    44→	case agent.TurnEnd:
    45→		return handleLifecycleTurnEnd(ag, event)
    46→	case agent.Compaction:
    47→		return handleLifecycleCompaction(ag, event)
    48→	case agent.SessionEnd:
    49→		return handleLifecycleSessionEnd(ag, event)

   217→

    83→	// Use WriteTemporary to create the checkpoint
    84→	isFirstCheckpointOfSession := state.StepCount == 0
    85→	result, err := store.WriteTemporary(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteTemporaryOptions{
    86→		SessionID:         sessionID,
    87→		BaseCommit:        state.BaseCommit,
    88→		WorktreeID:        state.WorktreeID,
    89→		ModifiedFiles:     ctx.ModifiedFiles,
    90→		NewFiles:          ctx.NewFiles,
    91→		DeletedFiles:      ctx.DeletedFiles,
    92→		MetadataDir:       ctx.MetadataDir,
    93→		MetadataDirAbs:    ctx.MetadataDirAbs,
    94→		CommitMessage:     ctx.CommitMessage,
    95→		AuthorName:        ctx.AuthorName,
    96→		AuthorEmail:       ctx.AuthorEmail,
    97→		IsFirstCheckpoint: isFirstCheckpointOfSession,
    98→	})
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write temporary checkpoint: %w", err)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	// If checkpoint was skipped due to deduplication (no changes), return early
   104→	if result.Skipped {
   105→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   106→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   107→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   108→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   109→			slog.Int("checkpoint_count", state.StepCount),
   110→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   111→		)
   112→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last checkpoint)\n")
   113→		return nil
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Update session state
   117→	state.StepCount++
   118→
   119→	// Note: LastCheckpointID is intentionally NOT cleared here.
   120→	// It is set during condensation and used by handleAmendCommitMsg
   121→	// to restore checkpoint trailers on amend operations.
   122→
   123→	// Store the prompt attribution we calculated before saving
   124→	state.PromptAttributions = append(state.PromptAttributions, promptAttr)
   125→
   126→	// Track touched files (modified, new, and deleted)
   127→	state.FilesTouched = mergeFilesTouched(state.FilesTouched, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   128→
   129→	// On first checkpoint, record the transcript identifier for this session
   130→	if state.StepCount == 1 {
   131→		state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart = ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier
   132→	}
   133→
   134→	// Accumulate token usage
   135→	if ctx.TokenUsage != nil {
   136→		state.TokenUsage = accumulateTokenUsage(state.TokenUsage, ctx.TokenUsage)
   137→	}
   138→
   139→	// Save updated state
```

    61→func handleLifecycleSessionStart(ag agent.Agent, event *agent.Event) error {
    62→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "lifecycle"), ag.Name())
    63→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-start",
    64→		slog.String("event", event.Type.String()),
    65→		slog.String("session_id", event.SessionID),
    66→		slog.String("session_ref", event.SessionRef),
    67→	)
    68→
    69→	if event.SessionID == "" {
    70→		return fmt.Errorf("no session_id in %s event", event.Type)
    71→	}
    72→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(event.SessionID); err != nil {
    73→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid %s event: %w", event.Type, err)
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Build informational message
    77→	message := "\n\nPowered by Entire:\n  This conversation will be linked to your next commit."
    78→
    79→	// Check for concurrent sessions and append count if any
    80→	strat := GetStrategy()
    81→	if concurrentChecker, ok := strat.(strategy.ConcurrentSessionChecker); ok {
    82→		if count, err := concurrentChecker.CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(event.SessionID); err == nil && count > 0 {
    83→			message += fmt.Sprintf("\n  %d other active conversation(s) in this workspace will also be included.\n  Use 'entire status' for more information.", count)
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Output informational message
    88→	if event.ResponseMessage != "" {
    89→		message = event.ResponseMessage
    90→	}
    91→	if err := outputHookResponse(message); err != nil {
    92→		return err
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Fire EventSessionStart for the current session (if state exists).
    96→	if state, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(event.SessionID); loadErr != nil {
    97→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state on start: %v\n", loadErr)
    98→	} else if state != nil {
    99→		if transErr := strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStart, session.TransitionContext{}, session.NoOpActionHandler{}); transErr != nil {
   100→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: session start transition failed: %v\n", transErr)
   101→		}
   102→		if saveErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); saveErr != nil {
   103→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state on start: %v\n", saveErr)
   104→		}
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}

    16→// Compile-time interface assertions for new interfaces.

    18→
    19→// HookNames returns the hook verbs Cursor supports.
    20→// Delegates to GetHookNames for backward compatibility.
    21→func (c *CursorAgent) HookNames() []string {
    22→	return c.GetHookNames()
    23→}
    24→
    25→// ParseHookEvent translates a Cursor hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    26→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    27→func (c *CursorAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    28→	switch hookName {
    29→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    30→		return c.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    31→	case HookNameBeforeSubmitPrompt:
    32→		return c.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    33→	case HookNameStop:
    34→		return c.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    35→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    36→		return c.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    37→	case HookNamePreTask:
    38→		return c.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    39→	case HookNamePostTask:
    40→		return c.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    41→	case HookNamePostTodo:
    42→		// PostTodo is handled outside the generic dispatcher (incremental checkpoints).
    43→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    44→	default:
    45→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    46→	}
    47→}
    48→
    49→// ReadTranscript reads the raw JSONL transcript bytes for a session.
    50→func (c *CursorAgent) ReadTranscript(sessionRef string) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    52→	if err != nil {
    53→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	return data, nil
    56→}
    57→
    58→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    59→func (c *CursorAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    60→	lines, _, err := transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	var prompts []string
    66→	for i := range lines {
    67→		if lines[i].Role != transcript.TypeUser {
    68→			continue
    69→		}
    70→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    71→		if content != "" {
    72→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    73→		}
    74→	}
    75→	return prompts, nil
    76→}
    77→
    78→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    79→func (c *CursorAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    80→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	lines, parseErr := transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)
    86→	if parseErr != nil {
    87→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", parseErr)
    88→	}
    89→
    90→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
    91→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
    92→			continue
    93→		}
    94→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
    95→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
    96→			continue
    97→		}
    98→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    99→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   100→				return block.Text, nil
   101→			}
   102→		}
   103→	}
   104→	return "", nil
   105→}

   109→func (c *CursorAgent) parseSessionStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   110→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   111→	if err != nil {
   112→		return nil, err
   113→	}
   114→	return &agent.Event{
   115→		Type:       agent.SessionStart,
   116→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   117→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   118→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   119→	}, nil
   120→}
   121→
   122→func (c *CursorAgent) parseTurnStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   123→	raw, err := readAndParse[userPromptSubmitRaw](stdin)
   124→	if err != nil {
   125→		return nil, err
   126→	}
   127→	return &agent.Event{
   128→		Type:       agent.TurnStart,
   129→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   130→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   131→		Prompt:     raw.Prompt,
   132→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   133→	}, nil
   134→}
   135→
   136→func (c *CursorAgent) parseTurnEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   137→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   138→	if err != nil {
   139→		return nil, err
   140→	}
   141→	return &agent.Event{
   142→		Type:       agent.TurnEnd,
   143→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   144→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   145→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   146→	}, nil
   147→}
   148→
   149→func (c *CursorAgent) parseSessionEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   150→	raw, err := readAndParse[sessionInfoRaw](stdin)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return nil, err
   153→	}
   154→	return &agent.Event{
   155→		Type:       agent.SessionEnd,
   156→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   157→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   158→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   159→	}, nil
   160→}
   161→
   162→func (c *CursorAgent) parseSubagentStart(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   163→	raw, err := readAndParse[taskHookInputRaw](stdin)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		return nil, err
   166→	}
   167→	return &agent.Event{
   168→		Type:       agent.SubagentStart,
   169→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   170→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   171→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   172→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   173→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   174→	}, nil
   175→}
   176→
   177→func (c *CursorAgent) parseSubagentEnd(stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
   178→	raw, err := readAndParse[postToolHookInputRaw](stdin)
   179→	if err != nil {
   180→		return nil, err
   181→	}
   182→	event := &agent.Event{
   183→		Type:       agent.SubagentEnd,
   184→		SessionID:  raw.getSessionID(),
   185→		SessionRef: raw.TranscriptPath,
   186→		ToolUseID:  raw.ToolUseID,
   187→		ToolInput:  raw.ToolInput,
   188→		Timestamp:  time.Now(),
   189→	}
   190→	if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   191→		event.SubagentID = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   192→	}
   193→	return event, nil
   194→}
   195→
   196→// readAndParse reads stdin and unmarshals JSON into the given type.
   197→func readAndParse[T any](stdin io.Reader) (*T, error) {
   198→	data, err := io.ReadAll(stdin)
   199→	if err != nil {
   200→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read hook input: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→	if len(data) == 0 {
   203→		return nil, errors.New("empty hook input")
   204→	}
   205→	var result T
   206→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &result); err != nil {
   207→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→	return &result, nil
   210→}
